.. date: 2026-09-29-16-32-46
.. gh-issue: 158446
.. nonce: dToaPr
.. release date: 2026-10-13
.. section: Security

Fix a crash or incorrect output that could occur when formatting a
:class:`float` or :class:`complex` with a precision close to the platform's
``INT_MAX``.  :c:func:`PyOS_double_to_string` now raises :exc:`ValueError`
for any precision of that magnitude, regardless of presentation type or
value, as the format string parsers already did for precisions above
``INT_MAX``.

..

.. date: 2026-09-23-11-34-30
.. gh-issue: 156793
.. nonce: zC_AjF
.. section: Security

:mod:`asyncio`: :meth:`loop.start_tls() <asyncio.loop.start_tls>` and
:meth:`loop.create_connection() <asyncio.loop.create_connection>` now
validate the *server_hostname* argument if an :class:`ssl.SSLContext` is
passed with *check_hostname* set to ``True``.

..

.. date: 2026-09-16-14-05-19
.. gh-issue: 156793
.. nonce: Qa1T5Z
.. section: Security

:meth:`ssl.SSLContext.wrap_bio` now validates its *server_side*,
*server_hostname* and *session* arguments similar to
:meth:`ssl.SSLContext.wrap_socket`.

In particular, a context with :attr:`~ssl.SSLContext.check_hostname` enabled
and no *server_hostname* passed to :meth:`!wrap_bio` now raises
:exc:`ValueError` instead of completing a handshake that verified the
certificate chain without verifying the peer's identity, with no indication
that the check had been skipped.

..

.. date: 2026-09-10-13-38-11
.. gh-issue: 157265
.. nonce: -vYuMp
.. section: Security

In :mod:`tarfile`, when extracting a link falls back to extracting a member
of the archive, skip the member when the filter function returns None when
called with the extracted member's name replaced with the link's.

..

.. date: 2026-08-31-16-47-33
.. gh-issue: 157953
.. nonce: KxCF5N
.. section: Security

Update bundled `libexpat <https://libexpat.github.io/>`_ to version 2.8.5.

..

.. date: 2026-08-18-13-54-05
.. gh-issue: 156002
.. nonce: CcWXPP
.. section: Security

Bound the amount of data :mod:`zipfile` decompresses per read for members
compressed with bzip2, LZMA, or Zstandard, matching the existing limit for
deflate. A small archive member could previously expand into an unbounded
allocation even when read in small chunks.

..

.. date: 2026-08-14-11-55-00
.. gh-issue: 157579
.. nonce: Kq3Vt2
.. section: Security

Fix a race condition in the cleanup of :class:`tempfile.TemporaryDirectory`.
When working around file system permission errors, files are now removed
relative to open directory file descriptors instead of resolving their path
again, so that replacing a directory of the tree with a symbolic link can no
longer make the cleanup delete files outside of the temporary directory.
This addresses :cve:`2026-12345`.

..

.. date: 2026-08-13-13-08-11
.. gh-issue: 155999
.. nonce: Xt4rWq
.. section: Security

Fix the :mod:`tarfile` ``tar`` and ``data`` extraction filters creating
directories outside the destination for members whose name leaves the
destination and returns to it, such as ``../evil/../dest/sub/file``. The
containment check used the resolved path, but intermediate directories were
created from the name as given.

..

.. date: 2026-08-10-12-00-00
.. gh-issue: 156293
.. nonce: sNIcbk
.. section: Security

Fix a crash in :mod:`ssl` when an :attr:`~ssl.SSLContext.sni_callback`
switches a connection to another :class:`~ssl.SSLContext` and the context
that carries the callback is no longer referenced by the application.
Servers that keep their ``sni_callback`` context alive (the usual case when
it wraps the listening socket or is stored on the server object) were not
affected. This addresses :cve:`2026-19445`.

..

.. date: 2026-08-06-11-43-20
.. gh-issue: 155292
.. nonce: j4pHBO
.. section: Security

Change the :mod:`stringprep` module and :mod:`encodings.idna` codec to not
consider Unicode codepoint attributes beyond those defined in :rfc:`3454`.

..

.. date: 2026-07-31-16-20-17
.. gh-issue: 155694
.. nonce: SsxlKG
.. section: Security

Fix :cve:`2026-15806` by scoping :class:`~urllib.request.HTTPPasswordMgr`
credentials to the URL scheme, preventing credentials stored for an HTTPS
URL from being used for a matching HTTP URL, while URIs without a scheme
continue to match any scheme.

..

.. date: 2026-07-04-17-00-00
.. gh-issue: 153030
.. nonce: RovkP6
.. section: Security

Fixed quadratic complexity in incremental parsing of long unterminated
constructs (such as tags or comments) in :class:`html.parser.HTMLParser`,
which could be exploited for a denial of service.

..

.. date: 2026-06-30-13-24-13
.. gh-issue: 152674
.. nonce: -2QVoL
.. section: Security

The :class:`xml.etree.ElementTree.Element` methods
:meth:`~xml.etree.ElementTree.Element.findall`,
:meth:`~xml.etree.ElementTree.Element.iterfind` and
:meth:`~xml.etree.ElementTree.Element.find` avoid quadratic behavior when
using XPath index predicates (``[1]``, ``[last()]``, ``[last()-N]``) on XML
documents with many same-tag siblings.

..

.. date: 2026-06-25-20-44-20
.. gh-issue: 152216
.. nonce: VxiK5y
.. section: Security

Update bundled `libexpat <https://libexpat.github.io/>`_ to version 2.8.2.

..

.. date: 2026-06-23-14-19-30
.. gh-issue: 151987
.. nonce: 8mNIMf
.. section: Security

The :meth:`tarfile.TarFile.extract` method now applies the given filter when
it extracts a link target from the archive as a fallback.

..

.. date: 2026-06-23-13-28-16
.. gh-issue: 151981
.. nonce: xBHEcU
.. section: Security

In :mod:`tarfile`, seeking a stream now stops when end of the stream is
reached.

..

.. date: 2026-06-16-14-58-02
.. gh-issue: 151544
.. nonce: _bexVy
.. section: Security

:file:`Modules/Setup.local` is no longer used as a landmark to discover
whether Python is running in a source tree, as it could potentially affect
actual installs. The :file:`pybuilddir.txt` file is now the sole indicator
of running in a source tree.

..

.. date: 2026-06-10-13-08-19
.. gh-issue: 151558
.. nonce: mL74i2
.. section: Security

Fixed an vulnerability in the :mod:`tarfile` ``data`` and ``tar`` extraction
filters where crafted archives could create a symlink pointing outside the
destination directory. This was a bypass of :cve:`2025-4330`.

..

.. date: 2026-05-30-09-36-20
.. gh-issue: 150599
.. nonce: nlHqU-
.. section: Security

Fix a possible stack buffer overflow in :mod:`bz2` when a
:class:`bz2.BZ2Decompressor` is reused after a decompression error. The
decompressor now becomes unusable after libbz2 reports an error.

..

.. date: 2026-05-30-00-00-00
.. gh-issue: 150743
.. nonce: httpdos
.. section: Security

:mod:`http.client` now limits the number of chunked-response trailer lines
it will read to :attr:`~http.client.HTTPConnection.max_response_headers`
(100 by default), and the number of interim (1xx) responses it will skip to
100.  A malicious or broken server could previously stream trailer lines or
``100 Continue`` responses forever, hanging the client even when a socket
timeout was in use. Reported by ``@YLChen-007`` via GHSA-w4q2-g22w-6fr4.

..

.. date: 2026-05-18-17-46-00
.. gh-issue: 149835
.. nonce: EebFlk
.. section: Security

:func:`shutil.move` now resolves symlinks via :func:`os.path.realpath` when
checking whether the destination is inside the source directory, preventing
a symlink-based bypass of that guard.

..

.. date: 2026-05-11-21-15-07
.. gh-issue: 149698
.. nonce: OudOcW
.. section: Security

Update bundled `libexpat <https://libexpat.github.io/>`_ to version 2.8.1
for the fix for :cve:`2026-45186`.

..

.. date: 2026-05-10-18-05-32
.. gh-issue: 87451
.. nonce: XkKB6M
.. section: Security

The :mod:`ftplib` module's undocumented ``ftpcp`` function no longer trusts
the IPv4 address value returned from the source server in response to the
``PASV`` command by default, completing the fix for CVE-2021-4189.  As with
:class:`ftplib.FTP`, the former behavior can be re-enabled by setting the
``trust_server_pasv_ipv4_address`` attribute on the source
:class:`ftplib.FTP` instance to ``True``.  Thanks to Qi Deng at Aurascape AI
for the report.

..

.. date: 2026-05-08-02-18-54
.. gh-issue: 149474
.. nonce: ujQ-mu
.. section: Security

Fix the binary writer in :mod:`profiling.sampling` not firing the audit
(:pep:`578`) when creating the output file. The writer and the reader now
accept any path-like object. Patch by Maurycy Pawłowski-Wieroński.

..

.. date: 2026-05-03-21-00-00
.. gh-issue: 149486
.. nonce: tarflt
.. section: Security

:func:`tarfile.data_filter` now validates link targets using the same
normalised value that is written to disk, strips trailing separators from
the member name when resolving a symlink's directory, and rejects link
members that would replace the destination directory itself. This closes
several path-traversal bypasses of the ``data`` extraction filter.

..

.. date: 2026-04-27-16-36-11
.. gh-issue: 149079
.. nonce: vKl-LM
.. section: Security

Fix a potential denial of service in :func:`unicodedata.normalize`. The
canonical ordering step of Unicode normalization used a quadratic-time
insertion sort for reordering combining characters, which could be exploited
with crafted input containing many combining characters in non-canonical
order. Replaced with a linear-time counting sort for long runs.

..

.. date: 2026-04-26-19-30-45
.. gh-issue: 149018
.. nonce: a9SqWb
.. section: Security

Improved protection against XML hash-flooding attacks in
:mod:`xml.parsers.expat` and :mod:`xml.etree.ElementTree` when Python is
compiled with libExpat 2.8.0 or later.

..

.. date: 2026-01-16-11-58-19
.. gh-issue: 143927
.. nonce: aviFeG
.. section: Security

Normalize all line endings (CR, CRLF, and LF) to LF+TAB when writing
multi-line configparser values.

..

.. date: 2026-10-12-23-32-50
.. gh-issue: 159285
.. nonce: nlLuwQ
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a crash in a debug build when copying an empty :class:`dict` fails with
:exc:`MemoryError`. Patch by Christian Aurich Zanettini Martins.

..

.. date: 2026-10-11-01-41-31
.. gh-issue: 129724
.. nonce: Kq3vTz
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix the output of :mod:`marshal` for compiled code, and so the content of
``.pyc`` files, depending on what was imported or compiled before in the
process.  A :class:`frozenset` constant (or, in the :term:`free-threaded
build`, a :class:`slice` constant) used by several functions of a module was
copied for each of them when one of its strings was equal to a string which
was already interned.  The compiler now replaces the strings of the
constants by the interned ones before the code objects are created, while
equal constants are still one object.  Which strings are interned does not
change, except for the strings of :class:`slice` constants made only of
ASCII letters, digits and underscores, which are now interned in the default
build like those of other constants.

..

.. date: 2026-10-10-23-50-00
.. gh-issue: 159137
.. nonce: gcWalk
.. section: Core and Builtins

Speed up the garbage collector by untracking tuples and counting the
surviving objects while it looks for unreachable objects, which saves two
walks of the collected generation in every collection.

..

.. date: 2026-10-10-00-00-00
.. gh-issue: 159137
.. nonce: gcReuse
.. section: Core and Builtins

Avoid a second reachability analysis in the garbage collector when no
weakref callback, finalizer, or collectable-object debug output ran.
Finished generators, coroutines, and async generators can skip this analysis
even when finalized. Free-threaded builds also avoid a second stop-the-world
pause in this case.

..

.. date: 2026-10-06-14-12-49
.. gh-issue: 158918
.. nonce: TDVkZx
.. section: Core and Builtins

Reduce C stack usage of ``_PyModule_IsPossiblyShadowing``, which could
potentially cause stack overflows during module attribute access while
deeply recursing in agressively optimized builds.

..

.. date: 2026-10-06-05-12-21
.. gh-issue: 158803
.. nonce: iYTpRQ
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a crash in :meth:`bytes.join` and :meth:`bytearray.join` in the
:term:`free-threaded build` when another thread concurrently mutates the
list being joined. Patch by Christian Aurich Zanettini Martins.

..

.. date: 2026-10-05-03-25-30
.. gh-issue: 158824
.. nonce: h-hJj7
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix module-level :meth:`~module.__getattr__` being called before a module's
own lazy imports were resolved, which allowed it to shadow them.

..

.. date: 2026-10-05-01-59-22
.. gh-issue: 158820
.. nonce: 3KLyOT
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a crash when a :ref:`lazy import <lazy-imports>` is declared or resolved
by a finalizer running during interpreter shutdown.

..

.. date: 2026-10-04-11-59-33
.. gh-issue: 158592
.. nonce: _BpoeY
.. section: Core and Builtins

Skip the array reallocation when shrinking a small :class:`list`. Patch by
Donghee Na.

..

.. date: 2026-10-04-01-40-31
.. gh-issue: 158790
.. nonce: F3kQ7z
.. section: Core and Builtins

Speed up :meth:`list.insert` and item deletion via :keyword:`del` in
free-threaded builds by using :manpage:`memmove(3)`.

..

.. date: 2026-10-03-19-11-03
.. gh-issue: 130706
.. nonce: 01-O6U
.. section: Core and Builtins

Add a fast path to ``_Py_Dealloc()`` for non-GC objects. Patch by Donghee
Na.

..

.. date: 2026-10-03-16-14-26
.. gh-issue: 158583
.. nonce: 7EUMvS
.. section: Core and Builtins

:meth:`bytes.fromhex` and :meth:`bytearray.fromhex`: Fix uninitialized
memory read. Patch by Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-10-02-16-19-35
.. gh-issue: 158592
.. nonce: V6W21J
.. section: Core and Builtins

``del list[index]`` now triggers shrinking the list's internal array.

..

.. date: 2026-10-01-20-00-00
.. gh-issue: 158140
.. nonce: small
.. section: Core and Builtins

Resolve lazily imported sibling submodules independently, so accessing one
does not import another merely because its declaration came last.

..

.. date: 2026-09-30-10-12-41
.. gh-issue: 158316
.. nonce: Yv3kQe
.. section: Core and Builtins

On Free Threading, speed up :meth:`bytearray.decode` by decoding the
underlying storage directly instead of going through the buffer protocol.

..

.. date: 2026-09-30-01-30-58
.. gh-issue: 158486
.. nonce: cqmMwv
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix the PowerSort mask calculation in :meth:`list.sort` for very large
lists.

..

.. date: 2026-09-29-07-28-20
.. gh-issue: 158380
.. nonce: F0J0PO
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix incorrect type guards for ``dict`` reads and writes in the experimental
JIT.

..

.. date: 2026-09-28-18-58-27
.. gh-issue: 158364
.. nonce: jBqmTs
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix crash when :func:`sys._current_frames` or
:func:`sys._current_exceptions` is called while another interpreter is
running.

..

.. date: 2026-09-28-11-52-49
.. gh-issue: 158219
.. nonce: tNjBVV
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix :class:`bytearray` constructor: do not use a bytes object if its hash
value is already computed. Patch by Cody Maloney and Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-09-26-23-44-44
.. gh-issue: 158241
.. nonce: NZ9OSU
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a crash and possible code object corruption following a
:exc:`MemoryError` while compiling Python code.

..

.. date: 2026-09-26-15-39-32
.. gh-issue: 158254
.. nonce: qT7vRk
.. section: Core and Builtins

Reverse iterators over a :class:`dict` and its views now raise
:exc:`RuntimeError` if the dictionary's keys change during iteration, like
forward iterators, instead of yielding entries for the new keys.

..

.. date: 2026-09-25-22-34-50
.. gh-issue: 158165
.. nonce: GVPxKy
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix compilation of ``_Py_get_machine_stack_pointer()`` with MinGW on
Windows. Patch by Donghee Na.

..

.. date: 2026-09-24-18-44-40
.. gh-issue: 158072
.. nonce: mHveWw
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a JIT crash when a class is instantiated with their default arguments

..

.. date: 2026-09-24-18-20-00
.. gh-issue: 153569
.. nonce: diagnostics
.. section: Core and Builtins

Correct missing-brace error messages for multiline f-strings and t-strings.
Improve tokenization of UTF-8 input split across many readline calls.
Preserve source order when reading files with stateful encodings. Preserve
input errors when scanning string literals.

..

.. date: 2026-09-24-17-30-48
.. gh-issue: 157914
.. nonce: 5fVJob
.. section: Core and Builtins

Revert :gh:`146064`. The linked PyMutex change was causing a significant
performance regression in some cases.

..

.. date: 2026-09-22-12-00-00
.. gh-issue: 157838
.. nonce: brcMrg
.. section: Core and Builtins

Merge biased reference counts on behalf of threads that are detached instead
of waiting for them to attach again, in the free-threaded build.

..

.. date: 2026-09-20-12-11-01
.. gh-issue: 157840
.. nonce: EAo1Kp
.. section: Core and Builtins

Speed up calls to :func:`hasattr` and three-argument :func:`getattr` for
unassigned attributes in an object's :attr:`~object.__slots__` by roughly
6x.

..

.. date: 2026-09-20-08-59-48
.. gh-issue: 157833
.. nonce: 3b0bac
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix call specialization for C functions and methods with ``METH_CLASS``,
``METH_STATIC``, or ``METH_COEXIST`` flags, improving their call
performance.

..

.. date: 2026-09-19-12-00-00
.. gh-issue: 157778
.. nonce: fstring-brace
.. section: Core and Builtins

Include the line number of the opening brace in the :exc:`SyntaxError` for
an unclosed f-string or t-string replacement field when a matching quote on
a later line is interpreted as the start of an unterminated string.

..

.. date: 2026-09-18-20-24-43
.. gh-issue: 157468
.. nonce: zVIAva
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix the JIT optimizer folding a builtin name to a constant taken from the
interpreter's builtins dictionary even when the running function uses a
different ``__builtins__`` mapping, such as one created with
``vars(builtins).copy()``. The optimizer now only folds when the function's
builtins is the interpreter's, and the folded constant is guarded at runtime
so that another function sharing the same code object but a different
builtins mapping does not use it.

..

.. date: 2026-09-18-15-20-00
.. gh-issue: 157757
.. nonce: Kq3Lm7
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a lazy ``import a.b as c`` reading ``b`` off ``a`` instead of importing
the module ``a.b``.  It now binds the submodule, and raises
:exc:`ModuleNotFoundError` when no module backs the name.

..

.. date: 2026-09-16-15-02-11
.. gh-issue: 155695
.. nonce: Kf3xQa
.. section: Core and Builtins

Resolved names are now removed from :data:`sys.lazy_modules` more
consistently: a lazy import of an already loaded module is no longer
recorded, and reifying ``lazy from pkg import attr`` now discards the
``pkg.attr`` entry.

..

.. date: 2026-09-15-17-04-54
.. gh-issue: 155742
.. nonce: 2DUW_9
.. section: Core and Builtins

In debug mode at Python exit, check if immutable singleton objects have been
modified by mistake to detect bugs in C extensions. Patch by Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-09-14-23-16-26
.. gh-issue: 156939
.. nonce: oZqlSt
.. section: Core and Builtins

When Python is built in debug mode, :class:`bytes` and :class:`bytearray`
destructors now check if the trailing null byte has been overridden to
detect buffer overflow. Patch by Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-09-13-21-00-00
.. gh-issue: 153568
.. nonce: prefix-cache
.. section: Core and Builtins

Speed up parsing by reusing memoized rule prefixes across consecutive
grammar alternatives, avoiding repeated rule calls and cache lookups.

..

.. date: 2026-09-13-17-00-00
.. gh-issue: 153568
.. nonce: target-grammar
.. section: Core and Builtins

Speed up parsing by sharing expression rules with assignment and deletion
targets, and simplify comparison and keyword argument rules.

..

.. date: 2026-09-13-12-46-10
.. gh-issue: 157378
.. nonce: Rg7Tq2
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix ``SyntaxError.offset`` and ``SyntaxError.end_offset`` for the "Non-UTF-8
code starting with ..." error when a non-ASCII character precedes the
invalid byte on the same line. Patch by Shamil Abdulaev.

..

.. date: 2026-09-13-10-43-24
.. gh-issue: 156762
.. nonce: Kbf2mo
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix undefined behaviour in :class:`operator.methodcaller`: its
:c:member:`~PyTypeObject.tp_clear` slot function returned ``void`` instead
of ``int``, so the garbage collector called it through an incompatible
function type. Patched by Shamil Abdulaev.

..

.. date: 2026-09-12-22-29-51
.. gh-issue: 157377
.. nonce: O6WYlM
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix :func:`gc.get_count` on the free-threaded build resetting the
thread-local allocation counter that schedules automatic garbage collection.
A thread that called it while allocating could prevent cyclic garbage from
ever being collected.

..

.. date: 2026-09-12-16-40-00
.. gh-issue: 157361
.. nonce: anextpy
.. section: Core and Builtins

Implement :func:`anext` in Python instead of C, in a frozen ``_pybuiltins``
module. The awaitable returned by ``anext(it, default)`` is now a plain
coroutine, so introspection tools such as :func:`asyncio.print_call_graph`
can see through it into :meth:`~object.__anext__`.

..

.. date: 2026-09-12-12-00-00
.. gh-issue: 153568
.. nonce: parser
.. section: Core and Builtins

Reduce temporary allocations while parsing Python source.

..

.. date: 2026-09-10-08-21-07
.. gh-issue: 157247
.. nonce: Zv4bz2
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix :func:`sys.activate_stack_trampoline` allowing activation from a
subinterpreter while the JIT is enabled in the main interpreter.

..

.. date: 2026-09-10-02-50-14
.. gh-issue: 157242
.. nonce: LsqOUJ
.. section: Core and Builtins

If :meth:`bytearray.resize` or :meth:`bytearray.take_bytes` fails, leave the
:class:`bytearray` unchanged, instead of clearing it. Patch by Victor
Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-09-05-17-32-11
.. gh-issue: 156995
.. nonce: Tk3bYt
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix :class:`bytearray` sharing its buffer with the single-byte
:class:`bytes` object of the same value, so that writing to the bytearray
modified that :class:`bytes` object. This happened with
:meth:`bytearray.take_bytes` when exactly one byte remained, and on the
free-threaded build when a bytearray was shrunk to one byte from another
thread.

..

.. date: 2026-09-04-22-00-00
.. gh-issue: 156910
.. nonce: aBcD3f
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a deadlock in the :term:`free threaded <free threading>` build when
setting ``__abstractmethods__`` on a type while another thread holds the
type lock.

..

.. date: 2026-09-04-16-12-30
.. gh-issue: 156942
.. nonce: Qk7Rw2
.. section: Core and Builtins

:mod:`marshal` no longer replaces the exception raised while marshalling the
value with a generic ``ValueError("unmarshallable object")``.  Error
messages now name the type of the unsupported object and, for the types
supported only by newer data formats, the required version.

..

.. date: 2026-09-04-14-06-00
.. gh-issue: 156091
.. nonce: nested-comp
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a crash when compiling deeply nested inlined list, set, or dict
comprehensions. A :exc:`SyntaxError` is now raised when the nesting exceeds
the compiler's static block limit.

..

.. date: 2026-09-04-05-25-35
.. gh-issue: 156865
.. nonce: 7c5SBs
.. section: Core and Builtins

Raise :exc:`ValueError`'s for overflows, while trying to change
:class:`memoryview` elements with ``'f'`` and ``'Zf'`` format codes.  Patch
by Sergey B Kirpichev.

..

.. date: 2026-09-03-15-20-00
.. gh-issue: 156894
.. nonce: Rt9Bx4
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix the position of syntax errors which cover a range if the line contains
non-ASCII characters before the error.

..

.. date: 2026-09-03-12-40-00
.. gh-issue: 156886
.. nonce: Kx7mQt
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix :exc:`ZeroDivisionError` spuriously raised for a complex power with a
non-zero base when the phase of the result is infinite, e.g.
``1e300**1e308j`` or ``complex('inf')**1j``.

..

.. date: 2026-09-01-07-15-20
.. gh-issue: 155525
.. nonce: A7kP2m
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix quadratic-time tokenization of modules containing many f-strings or
t-strings.

..

.. date: 2026-08-31-10-45-11
.. gh-issue: 156689
.. nonce: Jq3xNv
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix an out-of-bounds read in :func:`compile` and :func:`ast.parse` when an
AST object is passed with ``mode='func_type'``.

..

.. date: 2026-08-27-13-26-50
.. gh-issue: 156459
.. nonce: Z8Zqik
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix cleanup on error in ``compiler_set_qualname``. Previously it was called
in ``_PyCompile_EnterScope``, after the scope had been entered, and this was
not reversed in case of an error.

..

.. date: 2026-08-26-10-00-00
.. gh-issue: 155725
.. nonce: tRcMal
.. section: Core and Builtins

:mod:`tracemalloc` no longer acquires the :term:`GIL` nor creates a
temporary thread state to trace memory allocations: traceback frames now
store plain UTF-8 strings instead of Python str objects, and tracebacks are
captured using the Python thread state already associated with the calling
thread, even if it is not attached. Threads without a Python thread state
record the traceback as ``<unknown>``.

..

.. date: 2026-08-26-01-08-38
.. gh-issue: 156371
.. nonce: USpwKG
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix missing ``PyRefTracer_DESTROY`` events for trashcan deferred objects.
Patch by Donghee Na.

..

.. date: 2026-08-24-12-00-00
.. gh-issue: 156310
.. nonce: pSqIter
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix memory safety issues in the :func:`iter` fallback for objects that
implement :meth:`~object.__getitem__` without :meth:`~object.__iter__`
(``PySeqIter_Type``).  Sharing an iterator between threads in the
free-threaded build could use the underlying sequence after it was freed,
and re-entrant exhaustion in the default build could decrement the
sequence's reference count twice.  Concurrent iteration may still see
duplicate or missing items, but it no longer corrupts the interpreter state.

..

.. date: 2026-08-23-21-30-00
.. gh-issue: 64862
.. nonce: Kx7Qm2
.. section: Core and Builtins

The :func:`iter` function now accepts the *stop_exception* parameter. The
created iterator stops when the callable raises the specified exception. The
second parameter is now named *stop_value* and can be passed by keyword.
:func:`aiter` now accepts the same *stop_value* and *stop_exception*
parameters, calling an asynchronous callable and awaiting the result.

..

.. date: 2026-08-23-12-00-00
.. gh-issue: 156114
.. nonce: Kq7fXz
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a crash in the perf trampoline when a code object has a name or filename
that cannot be encoded to UTF-8. Patched by Shamil Abdulaev.

..

.. date: 2026-08-21-18-49-12
.. gh-issue: 156204
.. nonce: ZusA7e
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a crash in :c:func:`PyErr_GivenExceptionMatches` when evaluating deeply
nested exception tuples. The recursion is now bounded, and exceeding the
limit is reported as an unraisable exception.

..

.. date: 2026-08-21-14-38-06
.. gh-issue: 148750
.. nonce: xBijjR
.. section: Core and Builtins

:mod:`encodings` is no longer frozen, due to issues importing submodules
when using a zipped standard library.

..

.. date: 2026-08-20-22-13-20
.. gh-issue: 156126
.. nonce: pXm4Qr
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a crash when importing a module whose name contains characters that
cannot be encoded to UTF-8 (such as lone surrogates) while :option:`-X
importtime <-X>` is enabled.

..

.. date: 2026-08-19-06-43-54
.. gh-issue: 144446
.. nonce: k3QzXa
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix thread safety of the :attr:`!generator.gi_frame`,
:attr:`!coroutine.cr_frame` and :attr:`!agen.ag_frame` attributes in the
free-threading build.

..

.. date: 2026-08-18-18-13-47
.. gh-issue: 156021
.. nonce: 3mK5c5
.. section: Core and Builtins

Empty :mod:`AST <ast>` node representations no longer include ``...`` when
the recursion depth limit is exceeded, making them easier to distinguish
from non-empty ones. Patch by Bartosz Sławecki.

..

.. date: 2026-08-18-14-19-51
.. gh-issue: 155254
.. nonce: 75TMlb
.. section: Core and Builtins

Avoid warning about missing standard library when using
:ref:`sys-path-init-_pth-files`.

..

.. date: 2026-08-17-15-13-14
.. gh-issue: 155978
.. nonce: 4ztALD
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a memory leak in the free-threaded build when setting or deleting a
special method (such as ``__repr__``) on a class that has many subclasses.

..

.. date: 2026-08-16-19-33-01
.. gh-issue: 148653
.. nonce: Kt3vQx
.. section: Core and Builtins

Forbid :mod:`marshalling <marshal>` recursive tuples, and fix a crash when
unmarshalling a self-referencing tuple.

..

.. date: 2026-08-15-20-31-20
.. gh-issue: 155823
.. nonce: OhNmzi
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a JIT bug where the side exit taken when a virtual iterator is exhausted
left a stale value on the stack, so the tier one interpreter resumed with
corrupted state and ``yield from`` raised :exc:`AttributeError` instead of
finishing normally.

..

.. date: 2026-08-14-19-00-00
.. gh-issue: 155141
.. nonce: call-kw
.. section: Core and Builtins

Compile pure-keyword calls with 16 to 29 keyword arguments using the faster
``CALL_KW`` instruction.  This includes common cases such as dataclass
constructors with many fields.

..

.. date: 2026-08-13-13-50-00
.. gh-issue: 155752
.. nonce: Rp7K2x
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a crash when a :class:`types.GenericAlias` argument gains a
``__typing_subst__`` hook after the alias parameters have been cached.

..

.. date: 2026-08-11-13-50-35
.. gh-issue: 155504
.. nonce: PsTqZm
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a crash when thread state creation fails before the thread state is
fully initialized.

..

.. date: 2026-08-11-06-04-26
.. gh-issue: 155526
.. nonce: W7ZHXu
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix spurious :exc:`OverflowError` for ``abs(nanj)`` in case :c:data:`errno`
was previously set to :c:macro:`!ERANGE` by some library call. Patch by
Sergey B Kirpichev.

..

.. date: 2026-08-11-01-45-24
.. gh-issue: 129752
.. nonce: Zo3OUx
.. section: Core and Builtins

Don't update adaptive counters in the free-threaded build when thread-local
bytecode is disabled (``-X tlbc=0``). Patch by Donghee Na.

..

.. date: 2026-08-10-11-20-00
.. gh-issue: 155515
.. nonce: Lp3qWc
.. section: Core and Builtins

Track the internal HAMT iterators, which back iteration over a
:class:`contextvars.Context`, with the garbage collector.  A reference cycle
running through such an iterator was never collected, leaking the whole
context it iterated over.

..

.. date: 2026-08-07-18-32-15
.. gh-issue: 155400
.. nonce: Kq3Vpt
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a deadlock in the free-threaded build between two threads assigning to a
special method of the same class.  While applying the type slot updates with
the world stopped, only the type lock was prevented from being released; the
type dict mutex could still be released and re-acquired, in the wrong order,
if the thread blocked on the stop-the-world mutex.

..

.. date: 2026-08-07-14-45-02
.. gh-issue: 155315
.. nonce: Mk9Fd2
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix :mod:`marshal` so that a ``frozendict`` referenced more than once in the
serialized data round-trips correctly, instead of failing to load with
:exc:`ValueError`. Patch by tonghuaroot.

..

.. date: 2026-08-07-13-40-12
.. gh-issue: 155363
.. nonce: Qk3Vt9
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a leak in the :term:`free-threaded build` when creating a thread state
fails after an internal QSBR slot has been reserved for it.  The slot could
never be reclaimed, so the QSBR array grew without bound across repeated
failures.

..

.. date: 2026-08-07-10-14-56
.. gh-issue: 154701
.. nonce: zulh2S
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix an infinite loop in JIT when a ``FOR_ITER`` side exit links an executor
back to itself.

..

.. date: 2026-08-02-17-55-29
.. gh-issue: 154937
.. nonce: m9IBba
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a data race on thread handle identifiers when ``_thread._shutdown()``
runs concurrently with the startup of non-daemon threads in the
:term:`free-threaded build`.

..

.. date: 2026-08-01-18-04-11
.. gh-issue: 120321
.. nonce: k3XvQb
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix thread safety of :term:`asynchronous generators <asynchronous
generator>` when iterated, closed or thrown into concurrently from multiple
threads on the :term:`free threading` build. Also make an ``athrow()``
awaitable single-use: reusing it after completion now raises
:exc:`RuntimeError` instead of resuming the generator again.

..

.. date: 2026-08-01-15-07-10
.. gh-issue: 154902
.. nonce: DIi6sf
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a crash when ``__conditional_annotations__`` is rebound to a non-set
object.

..

.. date: 2026-07-29-08-43-14
.. gh-issue: 151464
.. nonce: o_mtmz
.. section: Core and Builtins

Exclude invalid token ``<>`` from :mod:`tokenize` output. :exc:`SyntaxError`
for ``<>`` now suggests ``!=``.

..

.. date: 2026-07-28-10-00-00
.. gh-issue: 133931
.. nonce: fnQzxD
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix data races when setting attributes of function objects on the
:term:`free threaded <free threading>` build.

..

.. date: 2026-07-27-17-20-49
.. gh-issue: 154719
.. nonce: eI88Gs
.. section: Core and Builtins

Trailing whitespace in a t-string interpolation expression is now preserved
in :attr:`string.templatelib.Interpolation.expression`, up to the closing
``}`` or the conversion (``!``), format (``:``), or debug (``=``) delimiter.
Explicit line continuations following a debug ``=`` remain part of the debug
text and are excluded from
:attr:`~string.templatelib.Interpolation.expression`.

..

.. date: 2026-07-27-16-29-10
.. gh-issue: 154775
.. nonce: _ISRIk
.. section: Core and Builtins

When matching a complex literal in :keyword:`case` statements, an extraneous
``+`` sign (for example, ``1++1j`` or ``1-+1j``) is no longer allowed.

..

.. date: 2026-07-26-09-07-41
.. gh-issue: 154709
.. nonce: M2uZ76
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix an out-of-bounds access in reverse dictionary iterators when the
underlying dictionary is cleared and modified after the iterator is created.

..

.. date: 2026-07-25-17-35-14
.. gh-issue: 154695
.. nonce: aQWLLv
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix :class:`asyncio.Task` raising :exc:`AttributeError` when created with
``eager_start=True`` and no explicit *loop* argument.

..

.. date: 2026-07-25-12-44-13
.. gh-issue: 154044
.. nonce: GBa2wP
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a data race on a descriptor's ``__qualname__`` cache in the
:term:`free-threaded build`.

..

.. date: 2026-07-25-12-43-42
.. gh-issue: 154196
.. nonce: 0rAdob
.. section: Core and Builtins

Improve :exc:`AttributeError` messages from unresolved lazy imports. Patch
by Bartosz Sławecki.

..

.. date: 2026-07-24-13-15-31
.. gh-issue: 153809
.. nonce: JFA6GT
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix interpreter crash while deallocating objects of :class:`asyncio.Task` on

..

.. date: 2026-07-22-14-30-00
.. gh-issue: 154429
.. nonce: Qk9mZ2
.. section: Core and Builtins

Slightly speed up deallocation of objects that are not tracked by the
garbage collector, such as :class:`int`, :class:`float` and :class:`str`.

..

.. date: 2026-07-21-14-38-49
.. gh-issue: 145868
.. nonce: 7iGRvU
.. section: Core and Builtins

Suggest the closest valid feature name in the :exc:`SyntaxError` message

.. date: 2026-07-20-20-16-08

.. nonce: l961cA
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a crash when getting deeply nested ``__parameters__`` from a
:class:`types.GenericAlias` objects.

..

.. date: 2026-07-19-19-14-39
.. gh-issue: 154043
.. nonce: Kych7F
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a data race when iterating a shared :class:`types.GenericAlias` iterator
from multiple threads under the :term:`free-threaded build`.

..

.. date: 2026-07-19-16-18-25
.. gh-issue: 154014
.. nonce: CJsjVL
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a JIT assertion during interpreter shutdown by initializing ``vm_data``
fields for cold executors that bypass ``_Py_ExecutorInit()``.

..

.. date: 2026-07-19-12-33-27
.. gh-issue: 153932
.. nonce: lKw2bo
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix thread safety issue in the ``__reduce__`` method of
:py:class:`enumerate`.

..

.. date: 2026-07-17-22-03-39
.. gh-issue: 153881
.. nonce: oDa06s
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix potential data race when calling :meth:`~object.__getstate__` under the
:term:`free-threaded build`.

..

.. date: 2026-07-16-02-50-01
.. gh-issue: 153785
.. nonce: fJqPKC
.. section: Core and Builtins

:exc:`AttributeError`: The default error message is now generated from
``name`` and ``obj`` attributes when both are set and the exception was
constructed with no positional arguments, or with a single positional
argument equal to ``name``. Patch by Bartosz Sławecki.

..

.. date: 2026-07-14-20-19-48
.. gh-issue: 152075
.. nonce: V9Rwhp
.. section: Core and Builtins

Reduced lock contention during LOAD_GLOBAL bytecode specialization under
free threading.

..

.. date: 2026-07-13-13-05-00
.. gh-issue: 78959
.. nonce: Xy4a2B
.. section: Core and Builtins

Add the *order* parameter to :meth:`memoryview.cast`. With ``order='F'`` it
creates a zero-copy Fortran-contiguous (column-major) view of a flat buffer,
mirroring the *order* argument of :meth:`memoryview.tobytes`.

..

.. date: 2026-07-11-16-53-21
.. gh-issue: 153568
.. nonce: newlines
.. section: Core and Builtins

Speed up the tokenizer by copying source text verbatim when it contains no
carriage returns.

..

.. date: 2026-07-11-15-45-00
.. gh-issue: 153578
.. nonce: Qm4Zt9
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix an out-of-bounds write in :meth:`bytearray.extend` when the bytearray is
resized while the argument's :meth:`~object.__buffer__` is being acquired,
for example by another thread. Patch by tonghuaroot.

..

.. date: 2026-07-11-15-30-00
.. gh-issue: 153570
.. nonce: Kb7Xd2
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a use-after-free in :meth:`bytearray.take_bytes` when the argument's
:meth:`~object.__index__` method resizes the bytearray. Patch by
tonghuaroot.

..

.. date: 2026-07-11-15-07-49
.. gh-issue: 153568
.. nonce: identcache
.. section: Core and Builtins

Speed up the parser by caching repeated identifiers during a parse.

..

.. date: 2026-07-11-15-01-45
.. gh-issue: 153568
.. nonce: toktext
.. section: Core and Builtins

Speed up the parser by not materializing the text of tokens whose text is
never read.

..

.. date: 2026-07-11-14-59-17
.. gh-issue: 153568
.. nonce: leftrec
.. section: Core and Builtins

Speed up parsing of left-recursive rules by updating their memoization
entries in place.

..

.. date: 2026-07-11-14-58-24
.. gh-issue: 153568
.. nonce: stackchk
.. section: Core and Builtins

Speed up the parser by turning its per-rule C-stack check into a single
inline comparison.

..

.. date: 2026-07-11-14-57-19
.. gh-issue: 153568
.. nonce: memoflt
.. section: Core and Builtins

Speed up the parser by letting memoization lookups that cannot match return
immediately.

..

.. date: 2026-07-11-01-17-57
.. gh-issue: 153419
.. nonce: U8HJOJ
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix multiple :class:`bytearray` crashes and reference leaks caused by
skipping :meth:`~object.__init__` and broken state setup code.

..

.. date: 2026-07-09-13-28-27
.. gh-issue: 153354
.. nonce: It4Jj_
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a crash in compiler-generated ``__annotate__`` functions when the
annotation format comparison returned a non-boolean result.

..

.. date: 2026-07-08-13-23-11
.. gh-issue: 153298
.. nonce: wvcXxN
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fixes a data race in :class:`types.GenericAlias` ``__parameters__``
initialization on free-threading builds.

..

.. date: 2026-07-07-04-16-48
.. gh-issue: 153205
.. nonce: 5fE8QZ
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a potential :exc:`SystemError` during vector calls when memory
allocation fails. A :exc:`MemoryError` is now raised instead.

..

.. date: 2026-07-06-18-00-00
.. gh-issue: 153171
.. nonce: errloc1
.. section: Core and Builtins

Improve syntax error messages for misplaced ``not`` after an operator in
contexts where a generic "invalid syntax" error was previously reported,
such as ``1 << 2 + not x`` and ``1 * + not x``.

..

.. date: 2026-07-06-16-30-00
.. gh-issue: 153236
.. nonce: lazy-import-errors
.. section: Core and Builtins

Propagate exceptions raised while importing lazy submodules instead of
reporting them as missing attributes.

..

.. date: 2026-07-01-12-00-00
.. gh-issue: 102960
.. nonce: fR8kJa
.. section: Core and Builtins

:ref:`Frame objects <frame-objects>` now support :mod:`weak references
<weakref>`.  Patch by Łukasz Langa.

..

.. date: 2026-06-30-14-50-00
.. gh-issue: 152708
.. nonce: aBcDeF
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix AST generation asymmetry where unary positive ``+`` was implicitly
dropped in pattern match expressions, restoring parsing parity with unary
negative ``-``.

..

.. date: 2026-06-30-14-00-00
.. gh-issue: 152682
.. nonce: yId7e5
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix NULL pointer dereference in :func:`compile` when a reserved name (e.g.
``__classdict__``) is used as a type parameter name and memory allocation
fails while formatting the error message.

..

.. date: 2026-06-29-23-29-14
.. gh-issue: 152635
.. nonce: O21J0O
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a crash caused when running out of memory creating a
:mod:`!_interpchannels` channel. Now a :exc:`MemoryError` is correctly
raised.

..

.. date: 2026-06-28-14-39-05
.. gh-issue: 152405
.. nonce: -_3YRo
.. section: Core and Builtins

Do not expose the internal mapping of :class:`types.MappingProxyType` when
performing rich-compare operations with non-stdlib types. Previously, it was
possible to mutate the internal mapping of a proxy there. Now we pass not
the original :class:`dict` instance, but its copy, when dealing with custom
types.

..

.. date: 2026-06-28-14-31-03
.. gh-issue: 152492
.. nonce: _09Zee
.. section: Core and Builtins

:type:`collections.OrderedDict` ``update`` method can now accept
:type:`frozendict` as an argument.

..

.. date: 2026-06-27-10-05-12
.. gh-issue: 152375
.. nonce: L-ZBk6
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix undefined behaviour when a :mod:`sys.monitoring` callback raised an
exception while the program was following a branch or loop.

..

.. date: 2026-06-26-22-38-21
.. gh-issue: 152192
.. nonce: lX2jIM
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a truncated ``oparg`` being passed to JIT trace initialization for a
``JUMP_BACKWARD`` with an ``EXTENDED_ARG``.

..

.. gh-issue: 152235
.. nonce: ZKWiWk
.. section: Core and Builtins

Defer GC tracking of :meth:`set.intersection`, :meth:`set.difference`,
:meth:`set.symmetric_difference`, :meth:`set.union` and ``set.__sub__``.
Patch by Donghee Na.

..

.. date: 2026-06-26-20-37-22

.. nonce: iVS7u5
.. section: Core and Builtins

:exc:`TypeError` messages for some instance methods called with too many

Defer GC tracking of a :class:`set` or :class:`frozenset` to the end of its

.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix an assertion failure when python is built in a debug mode that happened

.. nonce: K3jX9p
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix a crash when creating a :class:`dict` item iterator (for example

.. section: Core and Builtins

:meth:`!frozendict.fromkeys` now only tracks the :class:`frozendict` in the

.. nonce: DTKXjR
.. section: Core and Builtins

concurrent garbage collection cycle.  Access to the per-generation

consistent snapshot.

.. date: 2026-06-01-19-24-12
.. gh-issue: 150723
.. nonce: WlcL_-
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix perf jitdump timestamps on macOS. Events were stamped using
``CLOCK_MONOTONIC``, but macOS profilers timestamp their samples with
``mach_absolute_time()``. The mismatch prevented the JIT code mappings from
lining up with the samples, so no Python frame could be resolved.

..

.. date: 2026-06-01-19-21-01
.. gh-issue: 150723
.. nonce: Hb3JDG
.. section: Core and Builtins

Fix malformed perf jitdump thread ids on macOS. The ``thread_id`` field of
the ``JR_CODE_LOAD`` record was written as a 64-bit value instead of the
32-bit value required by the jitdump format, which shifted every following
field and prevented profilers from resolving Python frames.

.. date: 2026-10-11-08-41-00

Fix :func:`marshal.dumps` raising :exc:`TypeError` for a set or frozenset
containing several complex NaNs, which also made it impossible to write a
:file:`.pyc` file for source code containing such a constant.

..

.. date: 2026-10-11-08-40-00
.. gh-issue: 159185
.. nonce: Sq7cZd
.. section: Library

Fix a crash in :func:`marshal.dumps` and :func:`marshal.dump` when a set is
modified while its elements are serialized, for example by the
:meth:`~object.__buffer__` method of one of its elements.
:exc:`RuntimeError` is now raised.

..

.. date: 2026-10-11-06-00-00
.. gh-issue: 129724
.. nonce: mRsh1x
.. section: Library

The output of :func:`marshal.dumps` and :func:`marshal.dump`, and so the
content of :file:`.pyc` files, no longer depends on the reference counts of
the serialized objects: it is no longer changed by unrelated references to
them held elsewhere in the process nor, in the :term:`free-threaded build`,
by whether they have been used by other threads.  An object is now written
in a form that can be referenced later in the data if and only if it occurs
more than once in the serialized value; this also applies to interned
strings, which were always written in that form.  The format is unchanged
and the data can be read by previous Python versions that support the format
version, but it is not byte-for-byte identical to the data written by them.

..

.. date: 2026-10-08-16-18-43
.. gh-issue: 87720

Fixed error in :mod:`email` new policies where header folding failed to
detect the need to re-quote text obtained from encoded words in certain
cases.

call which is more efficient. In the good case, it makes ``readline()`` up
to 4x faster! Patch by Victor Stinner.

:func:`tempfile.TemporaryFile`, :func:`tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile`.

.. date: 2026-10-04-20-00-00

Wait for Windows threads to finish suspending before reading target memory
in the sampling profiler's blocking mode. Resume the target if waiting for
suspension fails.

..

.. date: 2026-10-01-16-45-00
.. gh-issue: 156321
.. nonce: Kq3xVt
.. section: Library

Fix :func:`asyncio.shield` reporting an exception from the inner future
through the loop exception handler as soon as it completes after the shield
was cancelled, even when the exception is retrieved afterwards. The
exception is now reported only if it is never retrieved, as with any other
future.

..

.. date: 2026-10-01-14-00-00
.. gh-issue: 158539
.. nonce: q1w2e3
.. section: Library

Fix :mod:`profiling.sampling` exception mode discarding samples for code
running inside an ``except`` block in a generator or coroutine. The remote
debugger now follows ``tstate->exc_info`` and its ``previous_item`` chain
instead of only reading the embedded ``exc_state``, matching the exception
that :func:`sys.exception` reports.

..

.. date: 2026-10-01-13-59-02
.. gh-issue: 158563
.. nonce: kR7wQe

Fix :func:`turtle.undo` after :func:`turtle.teleport` and after an exception
in :func:`turtle.circle`, :func:`turtle.dot` or :func:`turtle.write`. Fix
:func:`turtle.stamp`, :func:`turtle.clearstamp`, :func:`turtle.clear` and
:func:`turtle.reset` when the undo buffer is disabled.

..

.. date: 2026-09-30-22-10-00

Fix :mod:`profiling.sampling` not adding the directory containing the
profiled script to :data:`sys.path`, which prevented the script from

matching the behavior of ``python script.py``.

.. date: 2026-09-04-14-15-31

Don't raise exceptions on overflows in :class:`ctypes.c_float_complex`.
Patch by Sergey B Kirpichev.

.. date: 2026-09-04-09-18-05
.. gh-issue: 156920
.. nonce: lONsKT
.. section: Library

Fix :mod:`asyncio` on Windows: closing a :class:`~asyncio.DatagramTransport`
under :class:`~asyncio.ProactorEventLoop` while datagrams were still queued,

buffer, could strand the queued data and never call ``connection_lost()``,
hanging the close indefinitely.

.. section: Library

.. gh-issue: 156867

Raise :exc:`OverflowError`'s for native ``'Zf'`` format in
:func:`struct.pack`, like for ``'f'`` format.  Previously overflows in the
:c:expr:`float complex` type were silent.  Patch by Sergey B Kirpichev.

..

.. date: 2026-09-02-17-28-16
.. gh-issue: 156810
.. nonce: cLpEnc
.. section: Library

Fix a :exc:`UnicodeEncodeError` crash in the sampling profiler's
collapsed-stack export (``--collapsed``) when a sampled frame's function or
file name contains non-ASCII or surrogate-escaped characters. The output
file is now written as UTF-8.

.. nonce: sUbScp

The :mod:`symtable` module now represents inlined list, set and dict
comprehensions (:pep:`709`) as their own symbol table entries of type

..

.. date: 2026-09-01-16-58-30

.. nonce: Qm4tRs
.. section: Library

Fix :class:`xml.sax.saxutils.XMLGenerator`: the namespace URI is now escaped
in the namespace declaration.  Previously a URI containing ``&``, ``<`` or a
quote character produced a document which cannot be parsed.

..

.. date: 2026-09-01-02-30-00
.. gh-issue: 63102
.. nonce: Pz3wK8
.. section: Library

:class:`~xml.etree.ElementTree.XMLPullParser` and
:func:`~xml.etree.ElementTree.iterparse` now support the *target* parameter.
The reported object is the value returned by the corresponding method of the
target, so no tree is built if the target does not build one. Only the
standard :class:`~xml.etree.ElementTree.TreeBuilder` was supported in the C
implementation before.

..

.. date: 2026-09-01-00-30-00
.. gh-issue: 61290
.. nonce: Vx7pQ2
.. section: Library

:mod:`xml.etree.ElementTree` no longer refuses to serialize attributes
without a namespace when the *default_namespace* option is used. The default
namespace declaration does not apply to attribute names, so an unqualified
attribute name is written as is, and a qualified attribute name is always
written with a prefix.

..

.. date: 2026-08-31-23-10-00
.. gh-issue: 83895
.. nonce: Jm5tR3
.. section: Library

:mod:`xml.etree.ElementTree` now accepts input larger than 2 GiB in the C
implementation. The data is fed to Expat in chunks, as
:mod:`xml.parsers.expat` already did, instead of raising
:exc:`OverflowError`.

..

.. date: 2026-08-31-20-30-00
.. gh-issue: 99064
.. nonce: Rt4mZ9
.. section: Library

Fix :func:`xml.etree.ElementTree.parse` with a text file or other source of
:class:`str` data in the C implementation. The encoding declared in the
document was applied to the already decoded text, which produced mojibake.
It is now ignored, as when parsing with :meth:`!XMLParser.feed` or
:func:`~xml.etree.ElementTree.fromstring`.

..

.. date: 2026-08-31-19-30-00
.. gh-issue: 89499
.. nonce: Kx8vQ1
.. section: Library

:meth:`ElementTree.write() <xml.etree.ElementTree.ElementTree.write>`,
:func:`~xml.etree.ElementTree.tostring` and
:func:`~xml.etree.ElementTree.tostringlist` now support the *standalone*
parameter, the value of the standalone document declaration in the XML
declaration.

..

.. date: 2026-08-31-17-10-00

.. nonce: Nt2URL
.. section: Library

Fix :func:`!nturl2path.pathname2url` and :func:`!nturl2path.url2pathname`:
the filesystem encoding and error handler are now used for percent-encoding
and decoding, as in :mod:`urllib.request`.  Previously paths containing

.. date: 2026-08-31-11-40-00
.. gh-issue: 156774
.. nonce: Pd8Lz1
.. section: Library

Speed up :mod:`pdb` startup by only importing :mod:`asyncio` when the
program being debugged has already imported it.

..

.. date: 2026-08-31-09-01-22
.. gh-issue: 156439
.. nonce: j9WHCc
.. section: Library

available on platforms that provide them but not ``if_nameindex``, such as

..

.. date: 2026-08-31-00-00-00
.. gh-issue: 156698
.. nonce: gyDxUe
.. section: Library

Fix :class:`asyncio.ProactorEventLoop` UDP transports so that a write error
no longer strands a paused protocol: the write loop is now rescheduled when
data remains buffered, and the protocol is resumed when the buffer has
drained.

..

.. date: 2026-08-31-00-00-00
.. gh-issue: 127057
.. nonce: N9yg62
.. section: Library

Fix :class:`asyncio.ProactorEventLoop` UDP transports so that a
:exc:`ConnectionResetError` raised by ``WSARecvFrom`` no longer stops the
read loop.

..

.. date: 2026-08-30-21-00-00
.. gh-issue: 156665
.. nonce: Jm3Kp9
.. section: Library

:mod:`xml.dom.minidom` now validates namespaces in
:meth:`~xml.dom.Document.createElementNS`,
:meth:`~xml.dom.Document.createAttributeNS` and
:meth:`~xml.dom.Element.setAttributeNS`, and when setting
:attr:`~xml.dom.Node.prefix` of an attribute. :exc:`~xml.dom.NamespaceErr`
is now raised for a malformed qualified name, for a prefix with an empty
namespace, and for illegal use of the ``xml`` and ``xmlns`` prefixes.

..

.. date: 2026-08-30-19-30-00
.. gh-issue: 156658
.. nonce: Xq5Nt7
.. section: Library

:mod:`xml.dom` and :mod:`xml.etree.ElementTree` no longer treat characters
which are not white space in XML (such as U+00A0) as white space. Previously
they could be lost in :func:`~xml.etree.ElementTree.indent`,
:func:`~xml.etree.ElementTree.canonicalize` with ``strip_text=True``, and
when parsing with the ``whitespace-in-element-content`` feature turned off.

..

.. date: 2026-08-30-18-00-00
.. gh-issue: 81623
.. nonce: Vh2Kt6
.. section: Library

:meth:`~xml.dom.minidom.Node.toprettyxml` in :mod:`xml.dom.minidom` and
:func:`~xml.etree.ElementTree.indent` in :mod:`xml.etree.ElementTree` no
longer add whitespace inside an element which is marked with
``xml:space="preserve"`` or which contains text (:meth:`!toprettyxml` also
takes into account the content model declared in the DTD).  Previously such
indentation changed the content of the element.

..

.. date: 2026-08-30-13-37-47
.. gh-issue: 54092
.. nonce: Tq8Wm3
.. section: Library

Implement :meth:`~xml.dom.minidom.Node.writexml` for document fragments in
:mod:`xml.dom.minidom`, so that :meth:`~xml.dom.minidom.Node.toxml` and
:meth:`~xml.dom.minidom.Node.toprettyxml` now work for them. They write the
children of the fragment, without adding a level of indentation.

..

.. date: 2026-08-30-13-25-53
.. gh-issue: 156512
.. nonce: tVTOIV
.. section: Library

Fix :mod:`asyncio` losing a connection twice when ``resume_writing()``
closes the transport.

..

.. date: 2026-08-30-12-00-00
.. gh-issue: 44376
.. nonce: Kp4Vz9
.. section: Library

:meth:`~xml.dom.minidom.Node.writexml` in :mod:`xml.dom.minidom` now writes
the namespace declarations needed to serialize the namespaces of the element
and its attributes, if they are not already declared for an ancestor.  The
document is not modified.

..

.. date: 2026-08-29-11-37-40
.. gh-issue: 156545
.. nonce: fLaMe1
.. section: Library

Fix the sampling profiler's flamegraph export so that profiling a deeply
recursive program no longer fails with :exc:`RecursionError` instead of
producing a flamegraph. Patch by tonghuaroot.

..

.. date: 2026-08-28-18-10-25
.. gh-issue: 156523
.. nonce: llJqo9
.. section: Library

Fix :func:`asyncio.as_completed` not recording the awaiting task in the call
graph.

..

.. date: 2026-08-28-00-59-00
.. gh-issue: 156476
.. nonce: rQhxc7
.. section: Library

The pure Python implementation of :meth:`queue.SimpleQueue.get` now ignores
*timeout* when *block* is false, matching the documented behavior and the C
implementation. It previously raised :exc:`ValueError`.

..

.. date: 2026-08-27-08-31-29
.. gh-issue: 156444
.. nonce: klcySy
.. section: Library

Fix matching of a negated character set in a bytes pattern compiled with
both :const:`re.IGNORECASE` and :const:`re.LOCALE`.  The case closure is now
applied to the members of the set, so that ``[^bc]`` no longer matches
``b'B'``.

..

.. date: 2026-08-26-15-28-59
.. gh-issue: 156360
.. nonce: ctZMHf
.. section: Library

Add the :envvar:`PYTHON_TURTLE_LANG` environment variable to select the
language of :mod:`turtle` docstrings. Docstring dictionary entries naming a
method which does not exist in the running version are no longer reported.

..

.. date: 2026-08-26-13-58-01
.. gh-issue: 156400
.. nonce: dGrmP1
.. section: Library

Fix socket and pipe leaks in :mod:`asyncio` when ``protocol_factory()``
raises in :meth:`loop.create_datagram_endpoint
<asyncio.loop.create_datagram_endpoint>`, :meth:`loop.connect_read_pipe
<asyncio.loop.connect_read_pipe>`, and :meth:`loop.connect_write_pipe
<asyncio.loop.connect_write_pipe>`. The socket or pipe is now closed instead
of leaking until garbage collection.

..

.. date: 2026-08-26-12-48-10
.. gh-issue: 156408
.. nonce: -Yd0k7
.. section: Library

Fix :func:`asyncio.print_call_graph` raising :exc:`AttributeError` when
called on a task that has already finished.

..

.. date: 2026-08-26-08-20-07
.. gh-issue: 156404
.. nonce: 2gP33I
.. section: Library

Restore compatibility with :file:`.pth` files generated before Python 3.15
in the :mod:`site` module. Inject the ``fullname`` variable in the frame
which executes pth code.

..

.. date: 2026-08-26-02-30-00
.. gh-issue: 156353
.. nonce: abcdef
.. section: Library

Fix :mod:`configparser` parsing when using whitespace in *delimiters*.

..

.. date: 2026-08-24-22-20-55
.. gh-issue: 156327
.. nonce: arC8Ph
.. section: Library

Fix :func:`asyncio.print_call_graph` including its own frame in the printed
call stack.

..

.. date: 2026-08-24-14-28-47
.. gh-issue: 108549
.. nonce: XZ34WD
.. section: Library

Fix :class:`asyncio.Task`, when the task has a pending cancellation, replace
**exc** exception with a :exc:`asyncio.CancelledError` unless it is
:exc:`SystemExit` or :exc:`KeyboardInterrupt`, which must propagate
unchanged.

..

.. date: 2026-08-22-19-00-00
.. gh-issue: 156261
.. nonce: Nq4tWv
.. section: Library

:func:`pwd.getpwuid` now raises :exc:`OverflowError` instead of
:exc:`KeyError` if the user id is out of the range of the C ``uid_t`` type,
as :func:`grp.getgrgid` does for the group id.

..

.. date: 2026-08-22-14-12-40
.. gh-issue: 156230
.. nonce: Lt9wQd
.. section: Library

:meth:`curses.window.instr`, :meth:`~curses.window.in_wstr` and
:meth:`~curses.window.in_wchstr` no longer limit the count to 2047, which
silently truncated a longer line.

..

.. date: 2026-08-22-10-30-00
.. gh-issue: 156228
.. nonce: k3Rmxw
.. section: Library

The :mod:`ssl` and :mod:`hashlib` modules now route OpenSSL memory
allocations through the Python raw memory allocators, making OpenSSL memory
usage visible to :mod:`tracemalloc` and to custom allocators installed with
:c:func:`PyMem_SetAllocator`.

..

.. date: 2026-08-21-14-30-00
.. gh-issue: 156180
.. nonce: Qz3Lv8
.. section: Library

:func:`zlib.adler32_combine` and :func:`zlib.crc32_combine` now raise
:exc:`ValueError` if the *len2* argument is negative, instead of returning
rubbish or hanging indefinitely, respectively.

..

.. date: 2026-08-21-13-30-00
.. gh-issue: 156166
.. nonce: Xv8pQm
.. section: Library

:mod:`ssl`: A failed assignment or deletion of the ``_msg_callback``
attribute of :class:`ssl.SSLContext` no longer removes the current callback.
Deleting it now raises :exc:`AttributeError` instead of :exc:`TypeError`.

..

.. date: 2026-08-21-11-56-28
.. gh-issue: 156173
.. nonce: mhZa8a
.. section: Library

Calling :meth:`zlib.Decompress.flush` on invalid compressed data now raises
:exc:`zlib.error` instead of being silently ignored.

..

.. date: 2026-08-20-16-30-00
.. gh-issue: 156124
.. nonce: Kq3vXz
.. section: Library

Fix a crash in the free-threaded build when deleting the :attr:`!contents`
attribute of a :mod:`ctypes` pointer.

..

.. date: 2026-08-20-13-50-49
.. gh-issue: 156112
.. nonce: yEcma8
.. section: Library

:mod:`!_ios_support` no longer fails to import when
:func:`ctypes.util.find_library` cannot resolve the ObjC runtime through the
dyld shared cache, as is the case on iOS 13. The runtime is now loaded by
name, which dyld resolves against the cache directly.

..

.. date: 2026-08-20-12-10-00
.. gh-issue: 156101
.. nonce: Qb2xNv
.. section: Library

Fix :attr:`sqlite3.Cursor.arraysize` being set to 0 if the assigned value is
too large. The attribute is now left unchanged if the assignment fails.

..

.. date: 2026-08-20-12-05-00
.. gh-issue: 156100
.. nonce: Lm7qWz
.. section: Library

Fix crashes in :class:`sqlite3.Connection` when deleting the
:attr:`~sqlite3.Connection.autocommit` attribute or setting it to an integer
which does not fit in C :c:expr:`long`. Both now raise an exception.

..

.. date: 2026-08-20-12-00-00
.. gh-issue: 156099
.. nonce: Kp4vRt
.. section: Library

Fix a crash when deleting the ``keylog_filename`` attribute of
:class:`ssl.SSLContext`. It now raises :exc:`AttributeError`.

..

.. date: 2026-08-19-21-01-19
.. gh-issue: 156067
.. nonce: 1CXkqc
.. section: Library

Fix error handling in the :mod:`zoneinfo` accelerator module when a
transition index is ``-1`` or a TZ string's ``__bool__`` raises.

..

.. date: 2026-08-18-13-41-00
.. gh-issue: 155997
.. nonce: CjbD1F
.. section: Library

Fix :func:`concurrent.interpreters.list_all`.  It failed if an interpreter
was destroyed during the call, in particular by a garbage collection which
finalized the object owning the last reference to it.

..

.. date: 2026-08-17-22-51-22
.. gh-issue: 155974
.. nonce: Qz3Vb7
.. section: Library

Fix a regression in Python 3.15: :meth:`~curses.window.addstr`,
:meth:`~curses.window.addnstr`, :meth:`~curses.window.insstr` and
:meth:`~curses.window.insnstr` again restore the window attributes when the
write fails, instead of leaving the temporary *attr* applied.

..

.. date: 2026-08-17-21-00-00
.. gh-issue: 155941
.. nonce: strmCb
.. section: Library

Fix :func:`asyncio.start_server` when a plain-function *client_connected_cb*
raises: the error is now reported like the coroutine case and the transport
is closed, instead of leaving the connection open forever (which also made
:meth:`asyncio.Server.wait_closed` hang).

..

.. date: 2026-08-17-18-00-00
.. gh-issue: 155934
.. nonce: acCept
.. section: Library

Fix a socket leak in :mod:`asyncio` when transport creation fails for a
connection accepted by a server, and report the error via the loop exception
handler even when debug mode is disabled.

..

.. date: 2026-08-17-17-39-41
.. gh-issue: 155966
.. nonce: 0YOADY
.. section: Library

:func:`math.tanpi` now raises :exc:`ValueError` for half-integer values
(e.g., ``tanpi(1.5)``), as these are poles of the function.

..

.. date: 2026-08-17-12-40-00
.. gh-issue: 155952
.. nonce: Vq3Lm8
.. section: Library

Fix the signatures of :meth:`sqlite3.Connection.execute`,
:func:`warnings.warn` and :func:`locale.setlocale` which rendered
``<unrepresentable>`` instead of the correct defaults for parameters.

..

.. date: 2026-08-17-12-07-38
.. gh-issue: 155596
.. nonce: Rt4mZq
.. section: Library

Fix :func:`pprint.pprint` and :func:`pprint.pformat` with ``expand=True``
not honouring *width* for nested values.  The width of the ``'key':`` prefix
was not counted when deciding whether a value fitted on the current line, so
values under long keys could overflow *width* instead of being expanded.

..

.. date: 2026-08-16-11-38-17
.. gh-issue: 155888
.. nonce: pO-nAp
.. section: Library

Fix :meth:`asyncio.WriteTransport.writelines` hanging the transport when the
last data chunk is empty.

..

.. date: 2026-08-15-21-55-31
.. gh-issue: 155869
.. nonce: yRUnQW
.. section: Library

Fix :meth:`!reorganize` in :mod:`dbm.dumb` failing to persist updated value
offsets, which could cause data loss after reopening the database.

..

.. date: 2026-08-15-18-16-16
.. gh-issue: 121291
.. nonce: WljPkh
.. section: Library

:class:`enum.Flag` (and :class:`enum.IntFlag`) subclasses no longer have a
mixin base's own ``__or__``, ``__and__``, ``__xor__``, ``__ror__``,
``__rand__``, ``__rxor__``, or ``__invert__`` override silently replaced by
:class:`~enum.Flag`'s default implementation.

..

.. date: 2026-08-15-17-40-12
.. gh-issue: 155863
.. nonce: Kw3Vqp
.. section: Library

Add the ``WACS_*`` constants to the :mod:`curses` module.  They are the
counterparts of the ``ACS_*`` line-drawing codes as
:class:`curses.complexchar` cells, and are added, like the latter, by
:func:`curses.initscr` and :func:`curses.newterm`.

..

.. date: 2026-08-15-13-54-22
.. gh-issue: 155843
.. nonce: PD5Af3
.. section: Library

:mod:`hmac`: ensure that HMAC objects are properly initialized to prevent
rare crashes on allocation failures. Patch by Bénédikt Tran.

..

.. date: 2026-08-15-13-18-01
.. gh-issue: 155835
.. nonce: mlyLWp
.. section: Library

:mod:`hashlib`: Fix a data race when accessing
:attr:`~hashlib.hash.digest_size` on BLAKE-2 objects. Patch by Bénédikt
Tran.

..

.. date: 2026-08-15-13-12-09
.. gh-issue: 155835
.. nonce: DsbSmy
.. section: Library

:mod:`hashlib`: Fix data races when accessing
:attr:`~hashlib.hash.digest_size` and :attr:`~hashlib.hash.block_size` on
SHA-3 objects. Patch by Bénédikt Tran.

..

.. date: 2026-08-15-10-20-40
.. gh-issue: 155811
.. nonce: knP-YB
.. section: Library

Add a sequence counter to GC statistics to prevent :mod:`!_remote_debugging`
returning inconsistent snapshots caused by non-atomic reads. Patch by
Maurycy Pawłowski-Wieroński.

..

.. date: 2026-08-15-09-47-23
.. gh-issue: 155717
.. nonce: jWFLR2
.. section: Library

:mod:`multiprocessing`'s default start method is now :ref:`"spawn"
<multiprocessing-start-methods>` instead of ``"forkserver"`` when there is
no writable temporary directory, such as on a read-only file system.
Previously starting a process failed with an :exc:`OSError` there. Patch by
Bénédikt Tran.

..

.. date: 2026-08-14-14-10-00
.. gh-issue: 155775
.. nonce: Kq3vTx
.. section: Library

Fix a regression in :mod:`imaplib` introduced in the fix for :gh:`63121`:
refreshing the capabilities after a successful :meth:`~imaplib.IMAP4.login`
or :meth:`~imaplib.IMAP4.authenticate` consumed the ``CAPABILITY`` response,
so it could no longer be read with :meth:`~imaplib.IMAP4.response`.

..

.. date: 2026-08-14-12-46-07
.. gh-issue: 113318
.. nonce: DYGQjo
.. section: Library

Fix crashes when deleting an attribute whose setter is generated by Argument
Clinic and is not prepared for deletion, among them
:attr:`frame.f_trace_opcodes` and the ``context``, ``owner`` and ``session``
attributes of ``_ssl._SSLSocket``. Deleting such attribute now raises
:exc:`AttributeError`.

..

.. date: 2026-08-14-11-12-18
.. gh-issue: 155781
.. nonce: sqlite-converter
.. section: Library

Fix a possible crash in free-threaded builds when the :mod:`sqlite3`
converter registry is modified concurrently while a cursor builds its row
cast map.

..

.. date: 2026-08-14-10-49-45
.. gh-issue: 83371
.. nonce: Km4Rt7
.. section: Library

Fix a deadlock in :class:`multiprocessing.pool.Pool` when *callback* or
*error_callback* raises an exception. It killed the thread which handles
results, so that the pool hung forever. The exception is now the result of
the job, as an error raised while iterating the input, and is raised by
:meth:`!AsyncResult.get`.

..

.. date: 2026-08-13-16-02-13
.. gh-issue: 155702
.. nonce: Kb7Qm4
.. section: Library

Fix :class:`sqlite3.Blob` slice assignment with a step. It patched the bytes
object read from the blob, which for a single byte is an immortal singleton,
so that the value of that byte was changed in the whole process.

..

.. date: 2026-08-12-22-15-00
.. gh-issue: 90756
.. nonce: Qn7dLm
.. section: Library

:meth:`xml.etree.ElementTree.ElementTree.write` now treats the ``utf-8-sig``
encoding as ``utf-8`` in the XML declaration.

..

.. date: 2026-08-12-21-34-12
.. gh-issue: 44871
.. nonce: Rt7Nv2
.. section: Library

Parser objects of the :mod:`XML parser <xml.parsers.expat>` have a new
method :meth:`!GetSpecifiedAttributeCount`. It tells how many of the
attributes reported to :attr:`!StartElementHandler` were given in the start
tag rather than defaulted from the DTD.

..

.. date: 2026-08-12-21-34-12
.. gh-issue: 44871
.. nonce: Qw3Lm8
.. section: Library

:mod:`xml.dom.minidom` now conforms closer to the DOM Level 1 specification.
The factory methods now raise :exc:`~xml.dom.InvalidCharacterErr` if the
name is not a valid XML name. Removing an absent attribute with
:meth:`!Element.removeAttribute` or :meth:`!Element.removeAttributeNS` no
longer raises :exc:`~xml.dom.NotFoundErr`, it has no effect as the DOM
specifies. Inserting or setting a node created by another document now
raises :exc:`~xml.dom.WrongDocumentErr`, setting an attribute node which
belongs to another element now raises :exc:`~xml.dom.InuseAttributeErr`, and
inserting a node into itself or its descendant now raises
:exc:`~xml.dom.HierarchyRequestErr`. A document can no longer have two
element or two document type children, and a document fragment can no longer
have notation children. Attributes defaulted in the DTD are no longer
omitted when parsing, and :attr:`!Attr.specified` now reports whether the
attribute was given in the start tag. :class:`!EntityReference` nodes and
:meth:`!Document.createEntityReference` are now implemented.

..

.. date: 2026-08-12-14-30-00
.. gh-issue: 80762
.. nonce: Kp3vQa
.. section: Library

Fix :func:`dir` on :class:`unittest.mock.Mock` objects created with a tuple
*spec*: it raised :exc:`TypeError`. Such *spec* is now also documented.

..

.. date: 2026-08-12-09-30-00
.. gh-issue: 61366
.. nonce: Wr7cKm
.. section: Library

:meth:`!http.cookiejar.MozillaCookieJar.save` now writes ``0`` in the
expiration time field for session cookies, as curl and Wget do.  Previously
this field was left empty, and such lines were ignored by curl and Wget.

..

.. date: 2026-08-10-22-23-29
.. gh-issue: 153400
.. nonce: nucElC
.. section: Library

On Linux, :func:`os.copy_file_range` and :func:`os.memfd_create` now fall
back to the raw syscall when the libc Python is built against does not
provide the wrapper function, so they stay available on interpreters built
against a libc older than glibc 2.27.

..

.. date: 2026-08-10-22-11-15
.. gh-issue: 153400
.. nonce: Ds3GI3
.. section: Library

:mod:`os` and :mod:`signal`: Use glibc functions instead of ``syscall()``:
``pidfd_open()``, ``pidfd_getfd()`` and ``pidfd_send_signal()`` (glibc
2.36), ``gettid()`` and ``getdents64()`` (glibc 2.30), and ``getrandom()``
(glibc 2.25). Patch by Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-08-10-19-49-12
.. gh-issue: 155496
.. nonce: tJtiAt
.. section: Library

The ``ctypes.set_errno`` and ``ctypes.set_last_error`` auditing events are
now raised after the argument is converted to an integer, therefore they are
no longer raised if the argument is invalid.

..

.. date: 2026-08-10-19-49-11
.. gh-issue: 155496
.. nonce: B4rMR1
.. section: Library

:meth:`io.RawIOBase.readinto` and :meth:`io.RawIOBase.write` now raise
:exc:`TypeError` instead of :exc:`NotImplementedError` if they are called
without the required argument.

..

.. date: 2026-08-10-15-24-13
.. gh-issue: 155468
.. nonce: W7qL2p
.. section: Library

Fix :mod:`netrc` to distinguish empty quoted tokens from end-of-file, so
malformed files no longer cause the remaining content to be silently
ignored.

..

.. date: 2026-08-10-13-27-36
.. gh-issue: 155519
.. nonce: 9M6SFX
.. section: Library

Avoid a data-race in free-threaded builds when reading and writing context
variables from different threads.

..

.. date: 2026-08-10-09-00-18
.. gh-issue: 155336
.. nonce: vrTXoU
.. section: Library

Fix error handling in :func:`socket.gethostbyaddr` and
:func:`socket.gethostbyname_ex` when hostname resolution fails.

..

.. date: 2026-08-09-21-30-00
.. gh-issue: 118318
.. nonce: Xr4Kd8
.. section: Library

Remove the ``write_c14n()`` method of
:class:`xml.etree.ElementTree.ElementTree` and the support of
``method="c14n"`` in :meth:`~xml.etree.ElementTree.ElementTree.write` and
:func:`~xml.etree.ElementTree.tostring`.  They never worked and always
raised :exc:`ValueError`.  Use :func:`xml.etree.ElementTree.canonicalize`
instead.

..

.. date: 2026-08-09-16-14-38
.. gh-issue: 155433
.. nonce: KL7tHV
.. section: Library

Fix :class:`asyncio.TaskGroup` losing outside cancellation after
``cancel()``.

..

.. date: 2026-08-09-14-27-23
.. gh-issue: 155418
.. nonce: kCXUIG
.. section: Library

Fix :class:`asyncio.TaskGroup` hang when a task created by
:func:`asyncio.eager_task_factory` cancels the group before suspending.

..

.. date: 2026-08-09-01-30-00
.. gh-issue: 102475
.. nonce: rpjoin
.. section: Library

Fix :func:`os.path.realpath` on Windows: the unresolved part of the path is
now appended, not joined, so a file name which looks like a drive (e.g.
``spam:eggs``) no longer discards the resolved part.  A path relative to
another drive is now resolved against the root directory of that drive.

..

.. date: 2026-08-09-00-30-00
.. gh-issue: 89760
.. nonce: rpvol
.. section: Library

Fix :func:`os.path.realpath` on Windows: the ``\\?\`` prefix is no longer
stripped from a volume GUID path, which made the result invalid.

..

.. date: 2026-08-08-21-40-00
.. gh-issue: 84419
.. nonce: statexe
.. section: Library

Fix :func:`os.stat` on Windows: trailing dots and spaces, which are ignored
by the operating system, are no longer taken into account when the execute
permissions are guessed from the file extension.

..

.. date: 2026-08-08-18-30-00
.. gh-issue: 111331
.. nonce: bioclose
.. section: Library

Closing a :class:`io.BytesIO` object which has exported buffers no longer
fails with :exc:`BufferError`.  The exported buffers keep the data alive and
stay usable.  As a result, destroying or garbage collecting such object no
longer emits an unraisable exception.

..

.. date: 2026-08-08-09-14-22
.. gh-issue: 108885
.. nonce: C4ktfw
.. section: Library

Doctests run by the :mod:`unittest` test runner now report every example, as
in verbose mode, if the runner reports more than the test names, i.e. its
verbosity is 3 or higher.  The :option:`!-v` option of :mod:`unittest` can
now be repeated, so ``python -m unittest -vv`` asks for this.  Added also
the :attr:`~unittest.TestResult.verbosity` attribute of
:class:`unittest.TestResult`.

..

.. date: 2026-08-07-02-20-00
.. gh-issue: 155319
.. nonce: srcline
.. section: Library

:func:`warnings.warn_explicit` now displays the source line taken from the
loader of the module whose globals are passed as *module_globals*.  It also
no longer raises :exc:`IndexError` if *lineno* is out of the range of the
module source.

..

.. date: 2026-08-07-01-10-00
.. gh-issue: 123011
.. nonce: warnexp
.. section: Library

:func:`warnings.warn_explicit` no longer emits a spurious
:exc:`DeprecationWarning` or raises :exc:`ImportError` when it is called
with the globals of the :mod:`__main__` module.

..

.. date: 2026-08-05-17-30-00
.. gh-issue: 69370
.. nonce: inspCLI
.. section: Library

:program:`python -m inspect --details` no longer fails with
:exc:`UnicodeEncodeError` if the path of the module contains characters
unencodable in the encoding of :data:`sys.stdout`.  Such characters are now
escaped with backslashes.

..

.. date: 2026-08-05-16-40-00
.. gh-issue: 69371
.. nonce: pyDocU
.. section: Library

Fix :mod:`pydoc` for modules whose path contains undecodable bytes.
:func:`!pydoc.writedoc` and the pydoc HTTP server no longer fail with
:exc:`UnicodeEncodeError`: the file URL is now percent-encoded using the
filesystem encoding, and characters unencodable in the generated HTML page
are escaped with backslashes.

..

.. date: 2026-08-05-16-30-12
.. gh-issue: 155236
.. nonce: Kd7pQr
.. section: Library

Add the *dedent* parameter in :func:`inspect.cleandoc` and
:func:`inspect.getdoc`. :mod:`pydoc` no longer dedents documentation
strings, so the indentation of the parameter descriptions generated by
Argument Clinic is preserved.

..

.. date: 2026-08-05-02-36-18
.. gh-issue: 155009
.. nonce: YiJAiP
.. section: Library

Fix :class:`argparse.ArgumentParser` to preserve the program name from
``sys.argv[0]`` when a named module is executed as the main program without
replacing ``sys.argv[0]``.

..

.. date: 2026-08-04-19-50-52
.. gh-issue: 150942
.. nonce: B8ytes
.. section: Library

Improve the performance of :meth:`~io.IOBase.readlines` for
:class:`io.BytesIO`.

..

.. date: 2026-08-04-19-03-20
.. gh-issue: 155063
.. nonce: gN_mCU
.. section: Library

Bump the version of pip bundled in ensurepip to version 26.2.1

..

.. date: 2026-08-04-14-14-31

.. section: Library

On macOS, add run-time checks around the syscalls :manpage:`pipe2 (2)` and
:manpage:`dup3 (2)`, in addition to the existing build-time checks. This
means that Python built on macOS 27 (where these calls are available) can
run on macOS 26 (where they aren't).

:func:`decimal.localcontext` now raises :exc:`TypeError` if a keyword

.. section: Library

CSV dialects (instances of :class:`csv.Dialect` subclasses and dialect

.. date: 2026-07-30-23-29-56
.. gh-issue: 154936
.. nonce: a90443
.. section: Library

Fix the pure Python :mod:`json` decoder to report the correct position for
invalid literal control characters in JSON strings.

..

.. date: 2026-07-30-02-15-00
.. gh-issue: 154904
.. nonce: Kx9mAq
.. section: Library

Speed up :mod:`shutil` import by probing the ``_bz2``, ``_lzma`` and
``_zstd`` extension modules instead of importing the :mod:`bz2`, :mod:`lzma`
and :mod:`compression.zstd` wrappers, which are now only imported when an
archive is actually created or extracted.

..

.. date: 2026-07-29-21-37-39
.. gh-issue: 154892
.. nonce: eQyJ3Z
.. section: Library

Fix a bug in the C accelerator for :mod:`zoneinfo` where
:class:`datetime.datetime` subclasses returning ``-1`` for ``hour``,
``minute``, or ``second`` could incorrectly raise a :exc:`SystemError`.

..

.. date: 2026-07-29-19-48-59
.. gh-issue: 154871
.. nonce: tgZILS
.. section: Library

Fixed a crash in :meth:`asyncio.Task.get_context` when called on an
uninitialized task.

..

.. date: 2026-07-29-18-30-00
.. gh-issue: 154848
.. nonce: Fr4meB
.. section: Library

The :mod:`pickle` C accelerator now enforces frame boundaries when
unpickling, as the pure Python implementation already did.  An argument that
straddles a frame boundary, or a frame that begins before the previous one
has ended, now raises :exc:`pickle.UnpicklingError` instead of being
silently read across the boundary.  This prevents the loaded data from
diverging from the :mod:`pickletools` disassembly of the same pickle.

..

.. date: 2026-07-29-16-53-50
.. gh-issue: 154885
.. nonce: ptofmI
.. section: Library

:func:`os.get_terminal_size` now checks ``isatty`` before calling ``ioctl``,
which reduces log noise on Android.

..

.. date: 2026-07-29-14-20-40
.. gh-issue: 154855
.. nonce: vfF453
.. section: Library

Fix :meth:`curses.window.instr`, :meth:`~curses.window.in_wstr` and
:meth:`~curses.window.in_wchstr` returning one character too few when the
:mod:`curses` module is built against a curses library that counts the
terminator in the requested length, such as the NetBSD one.

..

.. date: 2026-07-29-11-58-17
.. gh-issue: 154874
.. nonce: NAPdBp
.. section: Library

Fix :func:`curses.termattrs` returning a negative value on a terminal that
supports :const:`curses.A_ITALIC`, which left its result unusable as an
attribute mask.

..

.. date: 2026-07-29-11-05-00
.. gh-issue: 151728
.. nonce: Kq3vTn
.. section: Library

Clear the internal :mod:`typing` caches from an exit handler. Previously, an
extension module that leaked a reference to :mod:`typing` would also keep
every subscripted type alive past interpreter shutdown, including types
owned by unrelated extension modules.

..

.. date: 2026-07-28-08-02-41
.. gh-issue: 154791
.. nonce: ey7POa
.. section: Library

Fix :class:`asyncio.Future` (C implementation) losing the initial exception
traceback frames when ``Future.result()`` is called more than once.
Previously, the C implementation cleared the stored traceback after the
first call, causing subsequent raises to omit the original raise site. The
pure-Python implementation was unaffected.

..

.. date: 2026-07-27-12-00-00
