Fix IDLE hanging after Restart Shell while receiving a large output from the
user process.

..

.. date: 2026-09-16-22-00-00
.. gh-issue: 70331
.. nonce: shadow
.. section: IDLE

IDLE no longer fails to start with ``python -m idlelib``, and its user
process no longer fails to start, when the current directory contains user
files with the same names as standard library modules that IDLE imports,
such as ``random.py`` or ``tkinter.py``.

..

.. date: 2026-09-16-20-00-00
.. gh-issue: 93016
.. nonce: cliarg
.. section: IDLE

Fix parsing of command line arguments in the IDLE "Run... Customized" dialog
on Windows: backslashes are no longer treated as escape characters. Fix also
display of the previous arguments in the dialog.

..

.. date: 2026-09-16-18-00-00
.. gh-issue: 59568
.. nonce: tilde1
.. section: IDLE

Fix "Save As" and "Open" dialogs in IDLE for a file whose name starts with
``~`` on Windows and X11 with Tcl/Tk older than 9.

..

.. date: 2026-09-15-23-27-38
.. gh-issue: 69004
.. nonce: Z7Ym9G
.. section: IDLE

The Debugger item of the IDLE Debug menu is now disabled while user code
runs, like Stack Viewer.

..

.. date: 2026-09-15-22-34-33
.. gh-issue: 69365
.. nonce: KehjZN
.. section: IDLE

The IDLE search dialogs now show a regular expression error below the entry
instead of in a message box.

..

.. date: 2026-09-15-22-10-17
.. gh-issue: 93966
.. nonce: ch4fTB
.. section: IDLE

The highlighting of a syntax error in the IDLE Shell is now removed when the
next statement is entered, not only when the next syntax error occurs.

..

.. date: 2026-09-15-21-54-04
.. gh-issue: 105689
.. nonce: ASoU37
.. section: IDLE

Fix calltips, parenthesis matching and auto-indent in the IDLE Shell after
output containing unbalanced quotes or parentheses.

..

.. date: 2026-09-15-21-44-35
.. gh-issue: 60402
.. nonce: iFz6X6
.. section: IDLE

In IDLE, Tab in a string that is not the start of a file name inserts a tab
instead of opening the completion list.

..

.. date: 2026-09-15-21-20-04
.. gh-issue: 68453
.. nonce: hlxbog
.. section: IDLE

Fix running IDLE with the -s option together with -c, -r or -: the command
or script now runs after the startup file, without an error dialog and
without restarting the shell.

..

.. date: 2026-09-15-20-14-08
.. gh-issue: 75234
.. nonce: O8ZNFV
.. section: IDLE

Fix editing help sources and key bindings in the IDLE Settings dialog after
selecting them with the keyboard.

..

.. date: 2026-09-15-16-37-19
.. gh-issue: 69919
.. nonce: f6AamT
.. section: IDLE

IDLE now reports any exception raised by compiling the source, such as
:exc:`MemoryError` for too deeply nested source, instead of hanging the
Shell.

..

.. date: 2026-09-15-16-19-25
.. gh-issue: 91398
.. nonce: PBdiDB
.. section: IDLE

Remove the border around the IDLE Shell sidebar, which was drawn in the
window background color and stood out with dark themes.

..

.. date: 2026-09-15-15-46-35
.. gh-issue: 71136
.. nonce: vwGO0v
.. section: IDLE

Fix a hang of the IDLE Shell when code run under the debugger raises
:exc:`KeyboardInterrupt`.

..

.. date: 2026-09-05-16-00-00
.. gh-issue: 154511
.. nonce: hV2wQn
.. section: IDLE

Consolidate IDLE's mouse wheel handling in ``idlelib.util``. ``wheel_event``
moves there from ``idlelib.tree`` and joins ``x11_buttons``, which tells
whether Tk reports wheel rotations to a widget as
``<Button-4>``/``<Button-5>`` events, and ``bind_wheel``, which binds
whichever events Tk sends.

..

.. date: 2026-09-03-17-16-26
.. gh-issue: 156896
.. nonce: XTQJlq
.. section: IDLE

Stop deleting tkinter submodules when idlelib.run is imported.

..

.. date: 2026-07-10-19-41-00
.. gh-issue: 153480
.. nonce: EdMt1x
.. section: IDLE

Fix IDLE failing to start when opening a file which does not exist yet. Fix
a traceback when a file open in the IDLE editor is deleted by another
program; IDLE now asks whether to close the window, save the file elsewhere,
or ignore it.

..

.. date: 2026-07-01-21-00-00
.. gh-issue: 55646
.. nonce: kBind9
.. section: IDLE

Fix IDLE crash at startup when the user configuration contains an invalid
key binding, such as ``<Alt-Key-up>`` instead of ``<Alt-Key-Up>``.  The
invalid binding is now ignored with a warning.

..

.. date: 2026-07-01-19-00-00
.. gh-issue: 82183
.. nonce: rNsTrt
.. section: IDLE

When the shell is busy running code, using "Run... Customized" with "Restart
shell" unchecked now reports that the shell is executing instead of
restarting it anyway.

..

.. date: 2026-07-01-18-00-00
.. gh-issue: 83653
.. nonce: cFgInt
.. section: IDLE

Blanking an integer entry in IDLE's Settings dialog, such as "Auto squeeze
min lines", no longer saves an empty string as an invalid configuration
value.

..

.. date: 2026-07-01-17-00-00
.. gh-issue: 65339
.. nonce: oUtTxt
.. section: IDLE

Saving the IDLE Shell or an Output window now defaults to a ``.txt``
extension and lists text files before Python files, since their content is
not Python source.

..

.. date: 2026-07-01-16-00-00
.. gh-issue: 80504
.. nonce: gReP
.. section: IDLE

The "In files:" field of IDLE's Find in Files dialog now always contains a
full directory path, even for an unsaved editor or the Shell.  This shows in
the grep output which directory was searched.

..

.. date: 2026-07-01-15-00-00
.. gh-issue: 134300
.. nonce: iDlPaT
.. section: IDLE

Do not add the ``idlelib`` directory to the path of the IDLE user process.
User code run in IDLE can no longer import ``idlelib`` submodules as
top-level modules, such as ``import help``.

..

.. date: 2026-07-01-14-00-00
.. gh-issue: 89360
.. nonce: mUlTiC
.. section: IDLE

Fix a rare crash in the IDLE editor when the completion window is closed:
deleting a key binding for a sequence that is not bound to the virtual event
is now ignored instead of raising a ``ValueError``.

..

.. date: 2026-07-01-13-30-00
.. gh-issue: 66172
.. nonce: Qm4xR7
.. section: IDLE

IDLE no longer fails to start when a user configuration file is corrupt. The
unparsable file is renamed with a ".bad" suffix, default settings are used
instead, and a warning lists the affected files.

..

.. date: 2026-07-01-12-00-00
.. gh-issue: 71956
.. nonce: rPlAlL
.. section: IDLE

Fix Replace All in the IDLE editor's Replace dialog when the search
direction is "Up" and "Wrap around" is off: it now replaces all matches
above the current position instead of only the first one.

..

.. date: 2026-07-01-00-15-58
.. gh-issue: 152728
.. nonce: yxIhMN
.. section: IDLE

Move functions run.fix_scaling, editor.fixwordbreaks (as fix_word_breaks)
and pyshell.fix_x11_paste to idlelib.util.

..

.. date: 2026-07-01-00-00-00
.. gh-issue: 66331
.. nonce: wMcLaS
.. section: IDLE

Set the ``WM_CLASS`` window property of IDLE's windows to ``Idle`` on X11,
so that window managers group and label them correctly instead of using the
default ``Toplevel``.

..

.. date: 2026-06-28-06-46-46
.. gh-issue: 85320
.. nonce: Hq2vKn
.. section: IDLE

IDLE now reads and writes its configuration files and the breakpoints file
using UTF-8 instead of the locale encoding.  This keeps non-ASCII data (such
as non-ASCII paths) from being corrupted and makes the files portable
between environments.

..

.. date: 2026-06-27-17-27-21
.. gh-issue: 90304
.. nonce: 464d22
.. section: IDLE

Prevent IDLE from failing to start when a font reports a zero width for the
``'0'`` character.  Such a broken font caused a :exc:`ZeroDivisionError`;
the editor now falls back to the configured width.

..

.. date: 2025-08-08-10-50-59
.. gh-issue: 135511
.. nonce: 9Rw5Zg
.. section: IDLE

Fix display of :exc:`NameError` and :exc:`AttributeError` with multi-line
message.

..

.. bpo: 6699
.. date: 2019-12-12-03-18-02
.. nonce: 1CqJFG
.. section: IDLE

Warn the user if a file will be overwritten when saving.

..

.. date: 2026-09-30-06-55-37
.. gh-issue: 158451
.. nonce: pZE56z
.. section: Tools/Demos

``python-gdb.py`` is now able to format an invalid Unicode string: render
invalid characters as ``\Uhhhhhhhh`` instead of raising an exception. Patch
by Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-08-23-18-10-00
.. gh-issue: 156261
.. nonce: Rw8pKd
.. section: Tools/Demos

Argument Clinic: the ``pid_t``, ``uid_t``, ``gid_t``, ``Py_off_t``,
``HANDLE``, ``DWORD`` and ``BOOL`` converters, previously defined in
individual files, are now provided by Argument Clinic itself.

..

.. date: 2026-08-22-11-00-00
.. gh-issue: 156219
.. nonce: Wq2nRt
.. section: Tools/Demos

Fix Argument Clinic generating code which reads an optional positional
argument which was not passed, if the function has a ``**kwds`` parameter.

..

.. date: 2026-08-14-13-15-35
.. gh-issue: 113318
.. nonce: oHjvUR
.. section: Tools/Demos

The value of a setter generated by Argument Clinic is now a parameter, which
can use a converter, and a getter can define a return converter. It is
optional to declare the value of the setter. The accessors of the same
attribute are now identified by the Python name of that attribute instead of
the C basename, so they can use different C basenames, and several
implementations of the same accessor can be defined in different
preprocessor conditional blocks.

..

.. date: 2026-08-14-12-45-57
.. gh-issue: 113318
.. nonce: G1B0oH
.. section: Tools/Demos

Fix Argument Clinic for ``@getter`` and ``@setter`` in a preprocessor
conditional block. It failed with an internal error. Argument Clinic now
also rejects the accessors of the same attribute with different C basenames,
and the same accessor defined twice, which silently generated invalid or
duplicated entries of :c:type:`PyGetSetDef`.

..

.. date: 2026-08-08-12-40-00
.. gh-issue: 155373
.. nonce: Wq3xTm
.. section: Tools/Demos

Argument Clinic now generates the inlined code of the converters for
functions with optional groups, instead of a ``PyArg_ParseTuple()`` call for
every number of arguments. Every argument is now parsed once, most such
functions now use the fastcall convention, and the errors for a wrong type
of an argument are the same as in other functions.

..

.. date: 2026-08-06-11-40-49
.. gh-issue: 64660
.. nonce: MswRQB
.. section: Tools/Demos

Argument Clinic return converters no longer need to hardcode the name of the
variable returned by the parsing function. It is now available as
``data.parser_retval``.

..

.. date: 2026-08-06-10-54-12
.. gh-issue: 155266
.. nonce: NEngp9
.. section: Tools/Demos

Fix Argument Clinic for a function whose only parameter is in an optional
group. It generated ``METH_O``, which made the argument mandatory and did
not pass the flag of the group.

..

.. date: 2026-08-06-09-43-16
.. gh-issue: 64595
.. nonce: W75XZK
.. section: Tools/Demos

Argument Clinic now touches the source file if a file generated from it was
changed, and keeps the generated files newer than the source file. The build
system does not know that the source file depends on the files generated
from it, so it did not recompile the source file if only the generated file
was changed.

..

.. date: 2026-08-06-08-32-53
.. gh-issue: 155263
.. nonce: RCcjtk
.. section: Tools/Demos

Add the ``--list`` option to Argument Clinic. It prints the modules, classes
and functions which Argument Clinic defines in the specified files, each
function with its signature.

..

.. date: 2026-08-05-18-56-17
.. gh-issue: 107570
.. nonce: wXxtw2
.. section: Tools/Demos

Argument Clinic: report errors on the offending line. Errors in a docstring
were reported on the line which ends the block, and errors detected when
generating the code were reported without any position.

..

.. date: 2026-08-05-18-16-24
.. gh-issue: 108270
.. nonce: kVDJSS
.. section: Tools/Demos

Argument Clinic: add support for deprecating a parameter with the ``[until
X.Y]`` marker. Passing such argument emits a :exc:`DeprecationWarning`.

..

.. date: 2026-08-05-18-16-23
.. gh-issue: 108271
.. nonce: Yvq2Tv
.. section: Tools/Demos

Argument Clinic: add support for parameter aliases. A keyword-only parameter
with a default value which shares the C name of a preceding parameter
declares an alternative name for it.

..

.. date: 2026-08-05-14-02-55
.. gh-issue: 155218
.. nonce: Nq4xZv
.. section: Tools/Demos

Fix Argument Clinic generating the flags of the optional groups in different
order on 32-bit and 64-bit platforms.

..

.. date: 2026-08-05-12-40-08
.. gh-issue: 155212
.. nonce: Lx8vTm
.. section: Tools/Demos

The ``--converters`` option of Argument Clinic now accepts file names and
can be used with ``--make``. It prints the converters and return converters
which the specified files define, instead of the built-in ones.

..

.. date: 2026-08-05-10-24-17
.. gh-issue: 155207
.. nonce: Wv3nKq
.. section: Tools/Demos

Argument Clinic now supports the ``--dry-run`` and ``--diff`` options. They
list the files which would be changed, or write a unified diff of the
changes to the standard output, without modifying any file.

..

.. date: 2026-08-04-19-05-41
.. gh-issue: 64502
.. nonce: Rb2wKt
.. section: Tools/Demos

Argument Clinic now supports several optional groups on the same nesting
level, like in ``[y, x,] [n,] attr``. Such groups can be omitted
independently of each other.

..

.. date: 2026-08-04-17-12-33
.. gh-issue: 64502
.. nonce: Qv7mLp
.. section: Tools/Demos

Fix Argument Clinic support of parameters with a default value used together
with optional groups. Such parameters were always required in the generated
parsing code.

..

.. date: 2026-07-24-09-30-00
.. gh-issue: 154580
.. nonce: Kp3nQ2
.. section: Tools/Demos

Fix ``python-gdb.py`` raising :exc:`UnicodeEncodeError` when pretty-printing
a non-ASCII :class:`str` in a locale whose host charset cannot encode it,
such as any non-ASCII string in the C locale.

..

.. date: 2026-07-19-00-00-00
.. gh-issue: 154059
.. nonce: JiBcOM
.. section: Tools/Demos

Fix the time units in Tachyon flame graph tooltips by accounting for the
sampling interval when converting samples to milliseconds.

..

.. date: 2026-07-16-11-38-15
.. gh-issue: 153804
.. nonce: KSTYg7
.. section: Tools/Demos

Add ``Tools/inspection/oracle_external_inspection.py``, a harness for
measuring the accuracy of Tachyon. It reports impossible-stack rates, speed,
error rates and the statistical distance from the blocking non-cached best
reference on selected snippets. Patch by Maurycy Pawłowski-Wieroński.

..

.. date: 2026-07-10-14-38-31
.. gh-issue: 152384
.. nonce: uaPADM
.. section: Tools/Demos

The CPython Pixi packages are now all accessible at the same
``Tools/pixi-packages`` subdirectory, rather than at
``Tools/pixi-packages/{variant}`` as before. Variants are now selected not
via subdirectory but via ``flags``; see
https://pixi.prefix.dev/latest/concepts/package_specifications/#extras-and-flags
for usage instructions. The ``tsan-freethreading`` variant has been renamed
to ``tsan_freethreading``, while the ``default``, ``asan``, and
``freethreading`` variants retain their previous names.

..

.. date: 2026-05-22-18-51-09
.. gh-issue: 150258
.. nonce: dh8GVK
.. section: Tools/Demos

Update the tooltip on the Tachyon flame graph to show both absolute and
relative percentages.

..

.. date: 2026-02-28-20-35-47
.. gh-issue: 87613
.. nonce: Nwzu6U
.. section: Tools/Demos

Add a ``@vectorcall`` decorator to Argument Clinic to generate
:ref:`vectorcall` parsing code for :func:`object.__init__` and
:func:`object.__new__`.

..

.. date: 2025-02-27-19-00-00
.. gh-issue: 130647
.. nonce: uwda2h
.. section: Tools/Demos

Add ``--omit-header`` option to :program:`pygettext`.

..

.. date: 2024-06-17-13-52-04
.. gh-issue: 111501
.. nonce: syFLAV
.. section: Tools/Demos

PS1 is no longer exported by venv activate script

..

.. date: 2026-10-11-06-20-11
.. gh-issue: 129724
.. nonce: Wq3nTb
.. section: C API

:c:func:`PyMarshal_WriteObjectToFile` now builds the marshalled data in
memory before writing it to the file, instead of writing it piece by piece
using a buffer of constant size.  Nothing is written any more if the object
cannot be marshalled.

..

.. date: 2026-10-05-17-09-35
.. gh-issue: 149044
.. nonce: 1HUiag
.. section: C API

Fix the :c:macro:`PySlot_END` macro for C++: initialize all members to avoid
compiler warnings. Patch by Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-10-05-15-24-22
.. gh-issue: 158842
.. nonce: g7eLtE
.. section: C API

Add :c:macro:`Py_SETREF` and :c:macro:`Py_XSETREF` macros to the limited C
API. Patch by Nathan Goldbaum and Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-10-05-01-09-32
.. gh-issue: 158810
.. nonce: F1p-PK
.. section: C API

Fix :c:macro:`Py_ARRAY_LENGTH()` macro on C++: do not use
``__builtin_types_compatible_p()`` on C++. Patch by Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-09-30-03-19-34
.. gh-issue: 158451
.. nonce: pUb2BJ
.. section: C API

Fix :c:func:`!_PyUnicodeWriter_WriteLatin1String` when the writer buffer
kind is UCS-2 or UCS-4. Patch by Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-09-29-19-01-56
.. gh-issue: 158439
.. nonce: ybKo_6
.. section: C API

If :c:func:`PyUnicodeWriter_WriteUTF8` or :c:func:`PyUnicodeWriter_Format`
fails, :c:func:`PyUnicodeWriter_Finish` now checks if the string uses the
most efficient storage. Previously, :c:func:`PyUnicodeWriter_Finish` could
return a string with an inefficient storage. Patch by Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-09-24-15-27-35
.. gh-issue: 158080
.. nonce: YIqypf
.. section: C API

Deprecate :c:func:`PyInitConfig_GetExitCode`, :c:func:`PyStatus_Exit`, and
:c:func:`PyStatus_IsExit` functions, and :c:member:`PyStatus.exitcode`
member. :c:func:`Py_InitializeFromInitConfig` and
:c:func:`Py_InitializeFromConfig` can no longer return an exit code.
Instead, if a command line option wants to exit Python, the option is now
processed in :c:func:`Py_RunMain`. Patch by Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-09-24-14-14-34
.. gh-issue: 154757
.. nonce: -b-Fs7
.. section: C API

:c:func:`PyModule_GetFilename` is no longer deprecated, but using
:c:func:`PyModule_GetFilenameObject` instead is still recommended. Patch by
Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-09-24-11-47-32
.. gh-issue: 157649
.. nonce: 3HSU2h
.. section: C API

Fix :c:macro:`Py_CLEAR` and :c:macro:`Py_SETREF` macros on C++: implement
them using ``auto`` instead of ``decltype()``. Using  ``decltype()``
produced invalid code when clearing/setting an array item. Patch by Victor
Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-09-23-23-52-55
.. gh-issue: 154757
.. nonce: EUVFeZ
.. section: C API

Remove :c:func:`!PyEval_InitThreads` function which did nothing since Python
3.7 and was deprecated since Python 3.9. Patch by Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-09-23-21-45-43
.. gh-issue: 158001
.. nonce: R_wX4E
.. section: C API

Remove :c:func:`!Py_SetProgramName`, :c:func:`!Py_SetPythonHome`,
:c:func:`!PySys_SetArgv` and :c:func:`!PySys_SetArgvEx` functions,
deprecated since Python 3.11. Patch by Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-09-23-19-37-53
.. gh-issue: 158001
.. nonce: X_Xamm
.. section: C API

Remove 21 global configuration variables:

* :c:var:`!Py_BytesWarningFlag`,
* :c:var:`!Py_DebugFlag`,
* :c:var:`!Py_DontWriteBytecodeFlag`,
* :c:var:`!Py_FileSystemDefaultEncodeErrors`,
* :c:var:`!Py_FileSystemDefaultEncoding`,
* :c:var:`!Py_FrozenFlag`,
* :c:var:`!Py_HasFileSystemDefaultEncoding`,
* :c:var:`!Py_HashRandomizationFlag`,
* :c:var:`!Py_IgnoreEnvironmentFlag`,
* :c:var:`!Py_InspectFlag`,
* :c:var:`!Py_InteractiveFlag`,
* :c:var:`!Py_IsolatedFlag`,
* :c:var:`!Py_LegacyWindowsFSEncodingFlag`,
* :c:var:`!Py_LegacyWindowsStdioFlag`,
* :c:var:`!Py_NoSiteFlag`,
* :c:var:`!Py_NoUserSiteDirectory`,
* :c:var:`!Py_OptimizeFlag`,
* :c:var:`!Py_QuietFlag`,
* :c:var:`!Py_UTF8Mode`,
* :c:var:`!Py_UnbufferedStdioFlag`,
* :c:var:`!Py_VerboseFlag`.

Patch by Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-09-22-23-20-53
.. gh-issue: 148233
.. nonce: U_2x-F
.. section: C API

Add :c:func:`Py_HashBuffer` to the limited C API. Patch by Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-09-18-00-29-49
.. gh-issue: 157710
.. nonce: Q_AD8D
.. section: C API

When Python is built in debug mode, :c:func:`PyUnicodeWriter_Finish` now
checks if the trailing null byte has been overridden to detect buffer
overflow. Patch by Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-09-17-23-18-12
.. gh-issue: 157710
.. nonce: 1xKQAH
.. section: C API

Soft deprecate functions modifying Unicode strings: :c:func:`PyUnicode_New`,
:c:func:`PyUnicode_CopyCharacters`, :c:func:`PyUnicode_Fill`,
:c:func:`PyUnicode_Resize`, :c:func:`PyUnicode_WRITE` and
:c:func:`PyUnicode_WriteChar`. Use the :c:type:`PyUnicodeWriter` API
instead. Patch by Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-09-16-21-24-36
.. gh-issue: 142217
.. nonce: 44sqt4
.. section: C API

Add tests on the undocumented and deprecated ``_Py_Identifier`` C API. Patch
by Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-09-14-18-07-43
.. gh-issue: 140550
.. nonce: fLVOGn
.. section: C API

Fix the :c:macro:`PyABIInfo_VAR` macro: remove redundant ``;``. Before,
``PyABIInfo_VAR(abi_info);`` code added two ``;;`` which is illegal in
C++03. Patch by Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-09-14-16-10-23
.. gh-issue: 157495
.. nonce: LzMLtE
.. section: C API

If ``typeof()`` is available, implement :c:macro:`Py_MIN`, :c:macro:`Py_MAX`
and :c:macro:`Py_ABS` using ``typeof()`` and statement expression to only
evaluate each argument once. Patch by Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-09-12-18-37-22
.. gh-issue: 157384
.. nonce: LGsy8K
.. section: C API

:c:func:`PyImport_CreateModuleFromInitfunc` now sets the package context
while calling *initfunc*, so that a single-phase init submodule created with
only the last component of its name gets the full name from the spec.

..

.. date: 2026-09-10-15-19-16
.. gh-issue: 155526
.. nonce: kMKhyp
.. section: C API

:c:func:`_Py_c_abs` no longer sets :c:data:`errno` to zero on success, but
rather leaves it unchanged.  Patch by Sergey B Kirpichev.

..

.. date: 2026-09-10-02-51-26
.. gh-issue: 157242
.. nonce: tfXIsf
.. section: C API

Fix :c:func:`PyBytesWriter_Resize` to handle properly memory allocation
failure. Leave the writer unchanged on error, instead of leaving it in an
inconsistent state. Patch by Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-09-04-16-41-07
.. gh-issue: 156939
.. nonce: bKaQuE
.. section: C API

When Python is built in debug mode, :c:type:`PyBytesWriter` now detects
buffer overflow.  Patch by Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-08-31-19-14-15
.. gh-issue: 156101
.. nonce: d4PSIF
.. section: C API

Don't set the result in error in :c:func:`PyLong_AsInt32`,
:c:func:`PyLong_AsUInt32`, :c:func:`PyLong_AsInt64` and
:c:func:`PyLong_AsUInt64`. Leave the result unchanged in this case. Patch by
Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-08-25-12-00-00
.. gh-issue: 131740
.. nonce: Ft7Rz2
.. section: C API

On the free-threaded build, :c:func:`PyUnstable_GC_VisitObjects` now also
visits frozen objects (objects moved to the permanent generation by
:func:`gc.freeze`), matching the behavior of the default build.

..

.. date: 2026-08-16-20-48-08
.. gh-issue: 155907
.. nonce: Vb3xTn
.. section: C API

:c:func:`PyMarshal_ReadObjectFromFile` and other functions reading
marshalled data from a :c:expr:`FILE*` now raise :exc:`OSError` for I/O
errors and :exc:`KeyboardInterrupt` for interrupted reading, instead of
:exc:`EOFError`. :c:func:`PyMarshal_WriteObjectToFile` and
:c:func:`PyMarshal_WriteLongToFile` now detect I/O errors and interrupted
writing instead of ignoring them. :c:func:`PyMarshal_WriteObjectToFile` now
also sets the error indicator if the value cannot be marshalled.

..

.. date: 2026-08-14-14-47-08
.. gh-issue: 155742
.. nonce: UGI3Pn
.. section: C API

:c:func:`PyBytesWriter_FinishWithSize` now returns single byte singletons if
*size* equals to ``1``. Patch by Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-07-30-16-58-36
.. gh-issue: 152105
.. nonce: 1Jz1YH
.. section: C API

Remove the :c:var:`!PyUnstable_ExecutableKinds` array, as well as the macros
:c:macro:`!PyUnstable_EXECUTABLE_KIND_SKIP`,
:c:macro:`!PyUnstable_EXECUTABLE_KIND_PY_FUNCTION`,
:c:macro:`!PyUnstable_EXECUTABLE_KIND_BUILTIN_FUNCTION`,
:c:macro:`!PyUnstable_EXECUTABLE_KIND_METHOD_DESCRIPTOR` and

..

If available on the platform, use native :c:type:`_Float16` in
:c:func:`PyFloat_Pack2` and :c:func:`PyFloat_Unpack2` functions.  Patch by
Sergey B Kirpichev.

..

.. date: 2026-07-09-17-33-29

.. nonce: Ma8MJZ

Fix :c:func:`Py_RunMain` to return an exit code, rather than calling
:c:func:`Py_Exit`, when running a script, a command, or the REPL. Patch by
Victor Stinner.

..

.. date: 2026-07-09-16-46-30

:c:func:`PyFloat_Pack8` and ``PyFloat_Unpack*`` functions are no longer
guaranteed to always succeed on CPython.

..

:c:func:`PyConfig_Set()` now also set global configuration variables. For
example, ``PyConfig_Set("inspect", value)`` now also sets
:c:var:`!Py_InspectFlag`. Patch by Victor Stinner.

..

..

object is not tracked by the garbage collector: if :func:`gc.is_tracked` is
false. Patch by Victor Stinner.
